| Model | Subject | Stage | Student manipulates | Gate | Eq / WP | Concept families |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `derivative-explorer` | math | plot | a point and a step h on f(x); the secant collapses to the tangent; f' is drawn beneath | numeric derivative vs mathjs symbolic derivative | no / no | derivative as a limit and as a function, average vs instantaneous rate, linear approximation, critical points, concavity, second-derivative test, mean value theorem |
| `integral-explorer` | math | plot | bounds and rectangle count; left, right, midpoint, trapezoid; the accumulation function | Riemann sums converge to the closed-form integral on test functions | no / no | Riemann sums, the definite integral, fundamental theorem of calculus, accumulation functions, area between curves |
| `taylor-series` | math | plot | centre and order; the polynomial hugs the function; an error band | coefficients vs symbolic derivatives; the Lagrange remainder bound holds | no / no | Taylor and Maclaurin series, Maclaurin series of common functions, Taylor error bounds |
| `slope-field` | math | plot | dy/dx = f(x, y); an initial point; Euler step size | Euler's method vs the exact solution on separable test equations; error shrinks linearly with step | no / no | slope fields, Euler's method, step size and error, autonomous equations and equilibria, the logistic differential equation |
| `solid-of-revolution` | math | 3D | a region and an axis; disk, washer or shell slices | numeric volume vs closed form (cone, sphere, paraboloid) | no / no | volumes of revolution, cylindrical shells, known cross sections, cross-sections and solids of revolution |
| `solid-geometry` | math, biology | 3D | the dimensions of a prism, cylinder, cone, pyramid or sphere; a cross-section plane | volume and surface-area formulas; Cavalieri equality | yes / yes | volume and surface area of solids, Cavalieri's principle, scale factor and area/volume ratios, cell size and surface-area-to-volume ratio |
| `conic-sections` | math | 3D | the tilt of a plane through a double cone; the cut curve and its equation | eccentricity from the tilt; the focus-directrix property holds on sampled points | no / no | conic sections from a cone, ellipses, hyperbolas, parabolas, eccentricity |
| `transformations-2d` | math | board | rotate, reflect, translate or dilate a figure; compose two | matrix form of each transform; distances preserved exactly for rigid motions | no / no | translations, reflections, rotations, dilations, rigid transformations, composing transformations, symmetry |
| `triangle-solver` | math | board | sides and angles of a triangle | angle sum; law-of-cosines consistency; 0, 1 or 2 solutions for SSA | yes / yes | Pythagorean theorem, SOH-CAH-TOA, solving right triangles, special right triangles, law of sines, law of cosines, the ambiguous case, area by trig |
| `parametric-polar` | math | plot | a parametric or polar curve; a moving point with its velocity vector; a complex-plane preset | arc length and speed vs closed form; polar/rectangular round trip | no / no | parametric equations, polar coordinates, polar/Cartesian conversion, vector-valued functions and motion, the complex plane, polar form, De Moivre's theorem |
| `vectors-3d` | math, physics | 3D | two or three vectors: sum, dot, cross, projection, span | dot and cross identities; projection residual is orthogonal | no / no | vectors and operations, magnitude and unit vectors, dot product, cross product, linear combinations and span, vectors and components |

### Wave 5: finish physics (18 models)

| Model | Stage | Student manipulates | Gate | Eq / WP | Concept families |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `kinematics-1d` | plot | initial position, velocity, acceleration; a cart moves under linked x-t, v-t, a-t graphs | x = x0 + v0 t + a t^2 / 2; v = v0 + a t | yes / yes | position, velocity and acceleration; motion graphs; kinematic equations; free fall |
| `collisions` | 3D | two masses, their velocities, the restitution coefficient | momentum conserved for every restitution; kinetic energy conserved only when elastic | yes / yes | conservation of momentum, elastic and inelastic collisions, momentum and impulse, centre of mass |
| `energy-conservation` | 3D | release height on a track or pendulum; mass; friction | speed from height, v = sqrt(2 g (h0 - h)); energy lost equals friction work | yes / yes | conservation of mechanical energy, kinetic and potential energy, work-energy theorem, energy in oscillators |
| `torque-balance` | 3D | weights and their positions on a pivoted beam | net torque and net force both zero at balance | yes / yes | torque, static equilibrium and torque balance |
| `rolling-motion` | 3D | shape (hoop, disk, sphere), mass, radius, incline angle | a = g sin(theta) / (1 + I / (m r^2)); translational plus rotational kinetic energy equals the potential energy lost | yes / yes | moment of inertia, Newton's second law for rotation, rotational kinetic energy and rolling |
| `angular-momentum` | 3D | a spinning platform; move masses in or out | I1 w1 = I2 w2; rotational kinetic energy changes by the work done moving the masses | yes / yes | angular momentum and its conservation, angular kinematics |
| `buoyancy` | 3D | object density and volume, fluid density | buoyant force = fluid density x g x submerged volume; floating fraction = density ratio | yes / yes | buoyancy and Archimedes' principle, fluid density and pressure |
| `fluid-flow` | 3D | pipe areas and heights, inlet speed | A1 v1 = A2 v2; P + rho v^2 / 2 + rho g h constant | yes / yes | continuity and Bernoulli's principle |
| `wave-superposition` | plot | amplitude, frequency and phase of two waves; their sum | nodes at half-wavelength spacing; beat frequency = abs(f1 - f2) | yes / no | wave properties, the travelling wave equation, interference and superposition, standing waves, beats |
| `doppler` | plot | source and observer speeds | f' = f (v + v_o) / (v - v_s) with signed speeds | yes / yes | Doppler effect |
| `electric-field` | plot | point charges placed on a plane; a test charge | E = k q / r^2 by superposition; V = k q / r | yes / yes | electric charge and Coulomb's law, electric fields, electric potential, dipoles and superposition |
| `magnetic-force` | 3D | charge, speed, field strength and angle | F = q v B sin(theta); r = m v / (q B) | yes / yes | magnetic force on moving charges |
| `magnetic-field-wire` | 3D | current and distance from a straight wire | B = mu0 I / (2 pi r); direction by the right-hand rule | yes / yes | magnetic fields from currents, electromagnetism |
| `refraction` | plot | incident angle and the two refractive indices | n1 sin(t1) = n2 sin(t2); critical angle = asin(n2 / n1) | yes / yes | refraction and Snell's law |
| `double-slit` | plot | slit separation, wavelength, screen distance | bright fringes at d sin(theta) = m lambda | yes / yes | interference and diffraction of light |
| `rc-circuit` | plot | resistance, capacitance, source voltage; charge or discharge | charging V0 (1 - exp(-t / RC)); discharging V0 exp(-t / RC); time constant = R C | yes / yes | RC circuits |
| `atomic-energy-levels` | board | initial and final level of a hydrogen atom | E_n = -13.6 eV / n^2; photon energy = h c / lambda | yes / yes | atomic energy levels and spectra |
| `photoelectric-effect` | board | light frequency and intensity; the metal's work function | KE_max = h f - phi; no electrons below the threshold frequency at any intensity | yes / yes | the photoelectric effect and photons |

`rc-circuit` is a separate model with a closed-form gate. The circuit builder's solver is
steady-state DC and is not extended to transients.

### Wave 6: statistics and computing (6 models)

| Model | Subject | Stage | Student manipulates | Gate | Eq / WP | Concept families |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `distribution` | math | plot | mean, standard deviation, a z interval; a binomial preset | normal areas 68.27, 95.45, 99.73 percent; binomial probabilities sum to 1 | yes / yes | the normal distribution and the empirical rule, z-scores, normal probability calculations, binomial distribution |
| `sampling` | math | plot | sample size, population shape; draw samples (seeded) | standard error = sigma / sqrt(n) | yes / no | sampling distributions, central limit theorem |
| `regression` | math | plot | draggable data points | least-squares slope = r s_y / s_x; residuals sum to zero | no / no | scatterplots and correlation, least-squares regression line, residuals and R-squared |
| `binary-numbers` | computing | board | a number; toggle bits | base conversion round trip across binary, decimal and hexadecimal | no / no | binary numbers, bits and bytes |
| `graph-explorer` | computing | board with `Stepper` | a small graph; a start node | breadth-first search visits nodes in non-decreasing distance; adjacency list and matrix describe the same graph | no / no | graph representation, breadth-first search |
| `logic-gates` | computing | board | a boolean expression | truth table by exhaustive evaluation; De Morgan equivalences hold | no / no | boolean logic and expressions |

Total: 65 new models across six waves (7, 12, 11, 11, 18, 6), on top of the 8 that exist:
73.

### Stage details

Colour. Before Wave 1 the palette gains what the new subjects need, in one place
(`materials.ts` and the `.viz` variables): a six-step series ramp for plots, a
positive/negative pair, a reactant/product pair, and element colours. Where a field has a
convention (red oxygen, red for positive charge) the hue follows the convention and the
lightness and saturation follow the house palette. Nothing is told apart by colour alone:
every coloured thing also has a label, a sign or a shape.

3D models, their one glass object, and what the 2D fallback shows:

| Model | Glass object | 2D fallback |
|---|---|---|
| `surface-3d` | the surface | contour map with the probe point and gradient arrow |
| `molecule-3d` | the central atom | wedge-and-dash structure with bond angles labelled |
| `solid-of-revolution` | the solid | the region and one slice in cross-section |
| `solid-geometry` | the solid | outline drawing with dimensions labelled |
| `conic-sections` | the cutting plane | the cut curve in its own plane with its foci |
| `vectors-3d` | the plane the vectors span | component table beside an x-y projection |
| `collisions` | the first cart | side view |
| `energy-conservation` | the ball | side view with the energy bars |
| `torque-balance` | the beam | side view |
| `rolling-motion` | the rolling body | side view |
| `angular-momentum` | the platform | top view |
| `buoyancy` | the fluid | side cross-section |
| `fluid-flow` | the pipe | side cross-section |
| `magnetic-force` | the charge | view along the field, showing the circular path |
| `magnetic-field-wire` | the wire | cross-section with the field circles |
| `ideal-gas` | the box | flat box of particles |
| `atom-model` | the nucleus | shell diagram |
| `crystal-lattice` | the unit cell | plan view, layer by layer |
| `stereochemistry` | the stereocentre | wedge-and-dash drawings of both mirror images |
| `conformations` | the front carbon | Newman projection |
| `cell-explorer` | the selected organelle | labelled cross-section |
| `dna-structure` | one base pair | untwisted ladder |

Second stages. These models declare an `aux` stage:

| Model | Main stage | Aux stage |
|---|---|---|
| `unit-circle` | the circle | the sine, cosine and tangent traces (plot) |
| `kinematics-1d` | the three linked graphs | the cart on its track (board) |
| `energy-conservation` | the track | the energy bars (board) |
| `ideal-gas` | the box of particles | the PV diagram (plot) |
| `states-of-matter` | the heating curve | the particles (board) |
| `conformations` | the molecule | the energy curve (plot) |

Board layouts, for the 17 board models:

| Layout | Models |
|---|---|
| `canvas` | `unit-circle`, `transformations-2d`, `triangle-solver`, `atomic-energy-levels`, `photoelectric-effect`, `galvanic-cell`, `reaction-mechanism`, `membrane-transport`, `cell-division`, `energy-pathways`, `graph-explorer` |
| `table` | `step-walkthrough`, `stoichiometry`, `gene-expression`, `genetics`, `binary-numbers`, `logic-gates` |

Gestures. Where the stage itself is the control: `linear-transform` (drag the tips of the
two basis vectors), `regression` (drag data points), `electric-field` (drag charges),
`unit-circle` (drag the point round the circle), `triangle-solver` (drag vertices),
`transformations-2d` (drag the figure). Each uses `DragHandle`, so each has arrow-key
nudging and a mirrored number field. The tilt of the plane in `conic-sections` is a
slider, because a drag there would fight with orbiting and with scrolling. Vectors in `vectors-3d` are edited by
their component fields, not dragged.

Boundaries that are the lesson, drawn and labelled rather than refused:

| Model | Boundary | What is drawn |
|---|---|---|
| `refraction` | past the critical angle | the reflected ray, labelled "total internal reflection" |
| `triangle-solver` | SSA with no solution, or two | no triangle with the gap shown, or both triangles |
| `doppler` | source at or above wave speed | the wavefronts piling into a cone; frequency shown as an en dash |
| `photoelectric-effect` | below the threshold frequency | no electrons at any intensity, labelled |
| `linear-transform` | determinant zero | the grid collapsed onto a line, labelled "no inverse" |
| `galvanic-cell` | negative cell potential | the cell labelled "not spontaneous as drawn" |
| `free-body-diagram` | static friction holding | the block at rest with friction equal to the pull |
| `ray-diagram` | object at the focal point | parallel rays, "image at infinity" (already built) |
| `orbit-sim` | at or above escape speed | the open path, labelled "unbound" (already built) |

Edges that range sampling will reach, and what each model does there:

| Model | Edge | What happens |
|---|---|---|
| `unit-circle` | tangent at 90 degrees | the tangent line runs off the stage; the value is an en dash |
| `linear-transform` | a matrix with no real eigenvectors (any rotation) | labelled "no real eigenvectors"; none drawn |
| `regression` | every point at the same x | no line; the slope is an en dash, labelled "vertical" |
| `triangle-solver` | three points in a line | the flat triangle, labelled "no triangle" |
| `electric-field` | the test charge on top of a source charge | the field there is an en dash |
| `conic-sections` | the plane through the tip of the cone | the point, line or pair of lines, labelled |
| `taylor-series` | a centre outside the function's domain | the centre's range stops at the domain's edge |
| `distribution`, `titration`, `galvanic-cell`, `equilibrium` | a zero spread or a zero concentration | the control's range starts above zero |
| `distribution` | a binomial with a large count | probabilities computed in logarithms, so they stay finite |

Permanent exclusion: the "Computer programming" course (JavaScript drawing, HTML/CSS and
SQL). Its concepts are language syntax and tooling; no model here shows them faithfully.
Every other course gets at least one mapped concept by the wave named in its pending entry.

## Order and delivery

- Wave 0 ships first, as the four stacked PRs in "Wave 0 in four PRs" (0.3.4.0 to
  0.3.7.0). No new model starts until all four are merged, because every later model
  depends on the folder shape, the frame's contract, the generator and the catalog.
- No PR carries more than seven new models, so one person can review it. Wave 1 is one
  PR; Wave 2 is two (6 and 6); Wave 3 is two (6 and 5); Wave 4 is two (6 and 5); Wave 5
  is three (6, 6, 6); Wave 6 is one. Each PR is one version bump (0.3.8.0 onward), through
  `/review` then `/ship`. That is 15 PRs in all.
- Inside a wave the models are split between its PRs in the order of the wave's table.
  The wave's kits and unit families land in its first PR, before the models.
  Integration (below) runs for each PR, not once per wave.
- The estimates are replaced with what the work actually took after 0b, and again after
  Wave 1, before Wave 2 starts.
- When the plan is approved, a dated note is added to `docs/DESIGN.md` recording the
  founder's decision to build the prebuilt model library past the original ~12 types, so
  the approved design and this plan do not contradict each other.
- Before a wave's fan-out the integrator builds any kit the wave first needs, adds the
  unit families it needs, creates each new model's branch with its folder scaffolded
  (`bun run new:model <type> --concepts ...`), and writes its brief from
  `docs/MODEL-BRIEF.md`.
- Inside a wave the new models are built in parallel (one agent per model, each in its own
  git worktree). A new model's branch touches only its own folder and runs
  `bun run test <type>`, `registry.test.ts`, build, lint, `bun run sweep <type>` and
  `bun run check:branch <type>`. Two things are not part
  of the fan-out and are done by one agent in sequence: kits (`src/engine/kits/`) and
  extensions to the existing 8 models, whose code lives in shared files.
- The integrator merges the folders, answers every kit request, deletes the exclusion
  entries the wave satisfied, then runs `bun run test`, `bun run test:catalog`, build,
  lint, `bun run test:e2e` and the full `bun run sweep` on the combined tree.
- A model that fails the second agent's review twice, or whose assigned concepts are
  all declined, is held out of its PR: its folder stays on its branch, the wave's entry
  in `docs/WAVES.md` names it with the reason, and the wave goes on. The founder decides
  later whether it is reworked or dropped. The catalog stays at 65 as the plan of record.
- Each PR is opened by `/ship` once CI is green, `/review` has passed and the second
  agent's reports are in. Work on the next PR starts at once, stacked on the open one;
  it does not wait for a merge. Whether a PR is merged and deployed without waiting for
  the founder is the founder's standing instruction, asked at approval; until the
  founder says so, a PR waits for the founder's merge. The founder's own checks
  (screenshots, reference tables) never block the next PR's work; what they find is
  fixed in the next PR.
- Waves run in the order above. The founder decided at approval (2026-10-01): all six
  waves, with no approval stop between them, and chemistry and biology straight after
  Wave 1. After 0b, after Wave 1 and after each later wave the integrator adds a dated
  entry to `docs/WAVES.md`: what shipped, what was held and why, real hours against the
  estimate, each kit request and its answer, and the usage numbers once the table
  exists. That file is how the founder is told. The work can still be stopped cleanly
  at any wave boundary with everything shipped up to that point.

## Effort

Agent time is the sum of work across parallel agents; wall-clock is elapsed time with four
to six models in flight at once.

| | Human team | CC + gstack agent time | CC + gstack wall-clock |
|---|---|---|---|
| Wave 0 (four PRs) | ~7 weeks | ~20 to 26 hours | ~20 to 26 hours |
| One model, to the recipe, with the second agent's check | ~2 to 3 days | ~60 to 100 min | n/a |
| Integration, sweep and hands-on pass, per wave | ~3 days | ~1.5 to 3 hours | ~1.5 to 3 hours |
| Wave 1 (7 models, presets, 5 extensions, `BoardStage` and `Stepper`) | ~6 weeks | ~12 to 17 hours | ~5 to 8 hours |
| Everything (Wave 0 and six waves: 65 models, 15 PRs) | ~11 to 13 months | ~115 to 165 hours | ~50 to 75 hours, across many sessions |

These are estimates made before any of it is built. They are replaced after 0b and again
after Wave 1.

## Risks

1. **Factual content outside physics.** Bond angles, standard potentials, the codon table,
   torsional barriers, mechanism steps and pathway steps come from reference knowledge, not
   from a derivation. Each such table gets cross-checks in tests where one exists (64
   codons, angle sums, charge balance, net yields), is checked against its cited source by
   a second agent, and is listed in the PR for a founder spot-check. With chemistry and
   biology now the second and third waves, most of this risk arrives early. Both checks
   apply to every model with reference data, starting with `molecule-3d` in Wave 1.
2. **Depth against breadth.** 65 models at the physics-model bar is a lot of surface to
   keep correct. The registry tests, the per-model closed-form tests and the sweep are what
   keep quality from decaying as the count grows.
3. **Simulated models** (`ParticleBox`, `natural-selection`, `sampling`) can show a picture
   that disagrees with the number beside it. Rule: every readout is closed-form; the
   animation is seeded and only illustrative; tests pin the seed.
4. **Bundle size.** The Try page loads the small meta index eagerly and one model's maths,
   presets and widget on demand; the topics page loads the catalog. `check:bundle` holds
   the line on every PR.
5. **The DESIGN.md target was ~12 spec types and "validate before building".** This plan
   goes to 73. The founder made that call at approval (2026-10-01): build all six waves.
   Both the strategy and the engineering reviewers advised stopping after Wave 1 to look
   at usage first; usage logging and the re-estimates after 0b and Wave 1 are what remain
   of that advice.
6. **Usage logging needs one founder action.** The logging code ships in Wave 0, but it
   records nothing until the founder runs `supabase/model_events.sql`. Until then the
   breadth is unmeasured.
7. **The frame's contract is new.** Every new model depends on how a widget and
   `ModelFrame` share the spec. It is proven on `projectile` and `function-grapher` in
   Wave 0, before any new model is built; a change after fan-out touches every model.
8. **The kits may not fit their consumers.** `PlotStage` starts as 126 lines and must
   serve about 25 models. Each kit is built against its hardest consumers first, and a
   kit request is the route for the rest.
9. **Search engines will not index concept pages.** The app uses hash routes, which are
   not indexed as separate pages. See "Not in scope".

## Not in scope

Deferred (each one that is real work has an entry in `TODOS.md`):

- Path routes with a title and description per concept, and prerendering, so concept
  pages can be found by search. This changes routing for the whole app, including the
  sign-in flow.
- A free-text entry point on the Try page with the classifier behind it. New models do not
  get a JSON-schema branch or a classifier-prompt paragraph until this exists: nothing
  would consume them. The original 8 keep theirs in `src/engine/spec.ts`, untouched.
- Shareable model state in the URL.
- Multi-step guided challenges per preset (each preset already has its one-line prompt).
- Moving the other six existing models' maths, equations and parsers into their folders
  (`projectile` and `function-grapher` are moved in Wave 0 as the reference models).
- The WebGL sweep in CI (headless GPU rendering is not dependable there; CI runs the 2D
  sweep and the WebGL sweep runs locally).
- More than one model per concept in the catalog (the other two channels, analogy and
  walkthrough, are not models; the shape changes when a second channel exists).
- A custom ESLint plugin for the colour rule (the import and raw-input rules are ESLint
  errors already; only the colour check is a script).
- A rate limit on `api/spec.ts` (the endpoint is switched off in 0a; a rate limit is a
  condition of switching it on).
- Converting the six legacy widgets to the full frame contract (they are wrapped in the
  frame's slots and keep their own state).
- Running only the changed models' sweep in CI, and a scheduled clean-up of old
  `model_events` rows (the report file carries the statement to run by hand).
- Crash reporting from students' browsers.
- A written design-token file for the project (the tokens live in `src/index.css` and
  `materials.ts`; a `/design-consultation` pass would turn them into one document).
- Mockups for this plan (the mockup generator has no API key on this machine).
- 3x3 matrices in `linear-transform`; 3D fields, 3D curl, line integrals and conservative
  fields in `vector-field`.

Not planned: the analogy and walkthrough channels (the other two "ways" per concept),
accounts and persistence beyond what exists, and the photo-of-a-problem input.

## Alternatives considered

- **Wave 0 only, then put it in front of students.** The cheapest way to learn which
  course pulls use. It is what DESIGN.md's validation step asks for. Not chosen as the
  default because the founder asked for the whole library; offered again at approval,
  where the founder chose all six waves.
- **Fewer concepts, three representations each** (the DESIGN.md thesis). Deeper per
  concept, far narrower. Not chosen: the request is the model channel across subjects.
- **Embed existing viewers** (an open-source molecule viewer for `molecule-3d`, a maths
  plotting library for the plot stage). Faster to first render, but each brings its own
  renderer, look and controls, and the gate-and-renderer-share-one-core rule would stop at
  the library boundary. The molecule scene is a few dozen spheres and cylinders on the
  existing `Scene3D`, and `PlotStage` is an extraction of code that already exists, so
  neither justifies a new dependency.
- **Physics first** (finish AP Physics 1 end to end before any other subject). Leads with
  the models that have the equation solver and the word-problem parser, which no free
  competitor has. Not chosen as the default because the request names the subjects with
  nothing built. At approval the founder chose the opposite order: chemistry and biology
  come straight after Wave 1.

## Reading the records below

The lists that follow this plan are the record of what each review accepted, in the order
the reviews ran. A later review sometimes replaced an earlier decision; each list says so
in its "Replaced" line. Where an earlier list disagrees with the sections above, the
sections above are current. Builder briefs are cut from `docs/MODELS.md` and
`docs/MODEL-BRIEF.md`, never from this file.

<!-- autoplan-accepted:ceo -->
- Record note: every requirement below is already written into the plan sections above. This list is the record of what the CEO review accepted, kept for later phases.
- Scope: 65 new models in six waves of 7, 12, 11, 11, 18 and 6 (Wave 1, chemistry, biology, calculus and geometry, physics, statistics and computing), plus five extensions to existing models; 73 models in total, delivered in 15 PRs of at most seven new models each (Wave 0 as four PRs, then 1, 2, 2, 2, 3 and 1 PRs for Waves 1 to 6), with the estimates replaced by Wave 1's actuals before Wave 2 starts. One primary interaction per model; variations are presets. `rc-circuit` is a separate Wave 5 model whose tests assert V0 * (1 - exp(-t / RC)) for charging, V0 * exp(-t / RC) for discharging, and a time constant of R * C; the circuit builder's steady-state solver is not extended. `linear-transform` is 2x2. `vector-field` is a 2D field with 2D curl and divergence.
- Goal: one gate and one report. The gate, applied by the curriculum build (`bun run gen:curriculum`): every course has a mapped concept or an entry in `exclusions.ts`; Wave 0 seeds one "pending Wave N" entry per uncovered course following the course-to-wave table in the plan, each wave deletes the entries it satisfies, and after Wave 6 only permanent exclusions with reasons may remain ("Computer programming" is the one known permanent exclusion); an entry for an already-mapped course or an unknown course also fails the gate. The report: per-course coverage and unmapped high 3D-fit concepts (230 are marked high-fit). Expected coverage of roughly 250 to 300 of 734 concepts is a report, not a target.
- Recipe: a TypeScript spec interface with named presets, shaped as a flat record of numbers, strings, enums and arrays of those; a one-sentence "try this" prompt in every preset's `notes`, shown on the Try page; LaTeX strings are code constants, never built from typed text; reference data in a `data.ts` with a cited source per table; a second agent that did not build the model repeats the hands-on pass and checks reference data against its source; one deterministic core shared by gate and renderer; an equation set and a word-problem parser exactly where the catalog's "Eq / WP" column says yes (a parser without an equation set only fills the widget's inputs); labels in demand-frameloop 3D scenes are HTML over the canvas or a legend beside it, never `Label3D`; one of three stages (3D, plot, board); keyboard-reachable controls; usable at 375px; one primary interaction; a test file with no node or bun APIs that includes "every preset validates"; verification by build, lint, the sweep page at 375px and 1280px with and without WebGL, and a hands-on pass.
- Engine shape (Wave 0): one folder per model with `meta.ts` (light, pure data), `manifest.ts` (presets and `validate`, pure), `core.ts`, optional `equations.ts` and `parse.ts`, `Widget.tsx`, `widget.css`, `concepts.ts` and a test file. `scripts/gen-models.mjs` (plain Node) writes `meta.gen.ts`, `load.gen.ts` and `concepts.gen.ts`, gitignored and produced by predev, prebuild, pretest and `gen:curriculum`; it registers a folder only when `meta.ts`, `manifest.ts` and `Widget.tsx` all exist, so a folder holding only a pre-assigned `concepts.ts` is ignored. `specTypes.ts` is derived from `meta.gen.ts` plus a static `PLANNED` list. `bun run test` runs every `src/**/*.test.ts`; `bun run test:catalog` runs the curriculum build in check mode and is run by the integrator on the combined tree, not by model branches. The existing 8 models get adapter folders (their example specs move from `VisualizePanel.tsx` into the manifests as presets), keep their internals and their two existing test files, and the five without tests (`projectile`, `wave-oscillator`, `free-body-diagram`, `circular-motion`, `function-grapher`) gain closed-form test files. Kits live in `src/engine/kits/` and are built by the integrator before the wave that first needs them (`PlotStage` Wave 0, exposing its scales, a pointer-to-data mapping and an overlay slot; `Stepper` Wave 1; `ParticleBox` Wave 2 as a seeded core with SVG and three renderers), together with any unit families the wave needs in `units.ts` (temperature stays in kelvin). The Try page has no model or preset picker. The Try page shows a stage-shaped skeleton while a model loads and a "This model didn't load" notice with retry when a chunk fails. A widget that throws shows "This model hit an error" with a link to topics. The tab title is the concept title or model label. A development-only `#/sweep` page mounts every model and preset inside an error boundary, with `?nowebgl` forcing the fallback. `safeExpression` in the kits is the only path from typed text to a function (mathjs parse, allowlisted nodes and function names, 200-character cap). `Stepper` stops at the last step, pauses on scrub and has no autoplay under reduced motion; `ParticleBox` caps at 400 particles; each kit's pure part has a unit test.
- Usage logging (Wave 0): `src/lib/usage.ts` records `open` and `engaged` (visible seconds, capped at 30 minutes, sent with keepalive on page hide) with model type, preset, concept id and a random per-tab session id, into a Supabase table `model_events` defined with insert-only row-level security in `supabase/model_events.sql`, which the founder runs once; a failed insert is dropped, warns once in development, and the coverage page shows "usage logging: on" or "table missing"; `supabase/usage_report.sql` reports opens and returning sessions per course per week. Tests: payload builder caps and drops; a rejected insert does not throw.
- Continuous integration (Wave 0): `.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs install, `bun run test`, `bun run test:catalog`, lint and build on pull requests; if the push is refused for lack of workflow permission the file is handed to the founder instead.
- Registry tests: `validateMappings` is a pure function tested on fixtures for each failure (unknown concept, duplicate mapping, missing preset, raw `specType`, uncovered course, stale or unknown exclusion); the generator's complete-folder rule is tested in `scripts/gen-models.test.ts`; meta types are unique and match their folder; preset ids in `meta.ts` and `manifest.ts` agree; every preset validates; the default preset exists; every mapped concept id exists; no concept maps to two models; every mapped preset exists.
- Mapping (Wave 0): a model's `concepts.ts` is the only place a concept is mapped. The 4 existing mappings move there; `specType` is removed from the raw curriculum files and the build fails if a raw file sets it. `bun run gen:curriculum` writes one committed catalog file per subject: courses in the order of `courseOrder.ts`, units in each course's `units` order, concepts in raw file order, each as id, title and optional `[type, preset]`; units with no concepts are omitted. Before each wave's fan-out the integrator writes each new model's `concepts.ts`; when two models could claim a concept it goes to the one whose primary interaction is that concept.
- Deep links (Wave 0): the resolution table in the plan, with `#/try` pinned to `projectile`, `?c=<conceptId>` winning over the path, unknown or unmapped ids ignored, an unknown preset falling back to the type's default, and an unknown type showing a "No model called X" notice with nothing rendered in its place. Header data comes from the lazily loaded subject catalog: with `c` present the stage shows its skeleton until the catalog resolves, and a failed load or an unknown subject prefix makes `c` unknown. The next link follows catalog order and skips unmapped concepts. Tests: a unit test of the resolver with one case per table row, and of the next-in-unit lookup (middle, last in unit, unknown id).
- Topics page (Wave 0): reads the generated catalog instead of Supabase; five subject tabs; search by case-insensitive title substring; an "interactive only" toggle; AND semantics; while either is active, units and courses with no match are hidden and courses with a match are expanded; "No concepts match" when the query is non-empty and nothing matches, "No interactive models in this subject yet" when the query is empty, the toggle is on and nothing matches; "N of M interactive" per course, counted unfiltered; the existing error line with a retry button when a catalog fails to load; Try links of the form `#/try/<type>/<preset>?c=<conceptId>`. Tests: unit tests of the pure filter and count functions (match, no match, empty query, mixed case, toggle alone, toggle with search), and a browser check that walks two next hops through "Forms of linear equations".
- `function-grapher` (Wave 0) is brought to the full recipe on `PlotStage` with presets for the eleven Algebra 1 concepts named in the plan and a test that every preset passes `validateFunctionGrapher` and that these eleven are finite at every sample across their domain.
- Existing-model extensions (Wave 1), each with assertions in that model's tests, new spec fields optional with defaults so `src/engine/spec.ts` schema and prompt text and `api/spec.ts` stay untouched: `circular-motion` gains mass and F = m v^2 / r; `wave-oscillator` gains F = k x; `circuit-diagram` gains named network presets and a test that Kirchhoff's current and voltage laws hold in the solver's result; `free-body-diagram` gains an optional static coefficient (defaulting to the existing `friction`, which stays kinetic), an optional applied force F and a flat-surface preset, holding while abs(m g sin(theta) - F) is at most mu_s m g cos(theta) and otherwise sliding with kinetic friction opposing the motion; `orbit-sim` gains an orbiting mass with F = M m / r^2 and U = -M m / r in natural units. One agent does the five in sequence.
- The coverage dashboard (`#/coverage`) uses the real mapping as the base layer; a real mapping wins and is read-only, and the localStorage planning layer applies only to unmapped concepts.
- Delivery: Wave 0 is version 0.3.4.0 and ships alone; each later PR (at most seven new models) is one version; new-model branches touch only their own folder and run their own test file, `registry.test.ts`, build, lint and the sweep for their model; the integrator merges, deletes satisfied exclusion entries, runs `bun run gen:curriculum`, then `bun run test`, `bun run test:catalog`, build, lint and the full sweep on the combined tree.
- On approval a dated note is added to `docs/DESIGN.md` recording the founder's decision to build the prebuilt library past the original ~12 types. The plan carries an "Alternatives considered" section.
- Deferred, to be written to TODOS.md when the plan is approved: path routes with per-concept titles and prerendering; a free-text entry point on the Try page with the classifier, including schema branches and prompt paragraphs for the new models; shareable model state in the URL; multi-step guided challenges per preset; migrating the 8 legacy models' internals into their folders; crash reporting from students' browsers; 3x3 matrices in `linear-transform`; 3D fields, 3D curl, line integrals and conservative fields in `vector-field`.
- Re-run after the approval gate (the founder's decisions, 2026-10-01): all six waves are built with no approval stop between them; after Wave 1 the order is chemistry (Wave 2), biology (Wave 3), calculus and geometry (Wave 4), the rest of physics (Wave 5), statistics and computing (Wave 6); the 65-model catalog is unchanged. `ideal-gas` and `nuclear-decay` are built in the chemistry wave, which fills the gases and nuclear chemistry units, so Wave 2 has 12 models and Wave 5 has 18. `solid-geometry` stays in Wave 4. `ParticleBox` is built before Wave 2. Courses first covered: Organic chemistry in Wave 2, AP Biology in Wave 3, AP Calculus AB, AP Calculus BC, Differential equations and Geometry in Wave 4. PRs per wave: 1, 2 (6 and 6), 2 (6 and 5), 2 (6 and 5), 3 (6, 6, 6) and 1. The advice to stop after Wave 1 was declined by the founder; usage logging and the two re-estimates remain.
- Re-run, pace and reporting: after 0b, after Wave 1 and after each later wave the integrator adds a dated entry to `docs/WAVES.md` (what shipped, what was held and why, real hours against the estimate, each kit request and its answer, usage numbers once the table exists). A model that fails the second agent's review twice, or whose assigned concepts are all declined, is held out of its PR and named there; the wave goes on and the catalog stays at 65 as the plan of record. `/ship` opens each PR once CI is green, `/review` has passed and the second agent's reports are in; the next PR's work stacks on it without waiting; a PR waits for the founder's merge unless the founder gives a standing instruction to merge without waiting; the founder's own checks never block the next PR's work. Inside a wave, models are split between its PRs in table order, the wave's kits and unit families land in its first PR, and integration runs per PR.
- Re-run, gate rules: in check mode a "pending Wave N" exclusion fails once every model planned for Wave N is built; `wave-oscillator`, `circuit-diagram`, `free-body-diagram` and `circular-motion` are named in a short exemption list that lets them pass check mode unmapped until the Wave 1 PR deletes it; a built model with no mapped concept is listed on the topics page in a "Not yet linked to a concept" row under its first subject, absent when empty; reference-data checks apply from `molecule-3d` in Wave 1.
- Re-run, Wave 0 assignments: 0a pins each default preset's readout values as `fmt` formats them, with no rendering, and the rendered text is checked by the 0d browser specs; the 0a tests keep the same assertions through 0b with only import paths changing, and the 0b PR lists any expression the old `function-grapher` check accepted that `safeExpression` refuses; 0b also carries `safeExpression`, the ESLint model rules, `check-models`, `check:branch` and the `function-grapher` Algebra 1 presets and mappings as data; 0c also carries the quantity reader, the KaTeX component, the palette additions, the single WebGL check, and the conversion of `projectile` and `function-grapher` to the frame's contract (`PlotStage` is extracted from `function-grapher`'s widget); 0d carries the browser walk through the Algebra 1 mappings and the scaffold with plot and 3D templates; the board template, its scaffold test and the board tab-order walk arrive with `BoardStage` in Wave 1, and `--stage board` is refused until then.
- Re-run, `planned.ts` inputs: where a wave's table has no Subject column the subject is the wave's own, with `ideal-gas` and `nuclear-decay` chemistry and physics; a model's label is its type in words; the 17 board models' layouts are listed in "Stage details" (11 `canvas`, 6 `table`). The message table gains one row (a pending entry that outlived its wave), 28 in all.
<!-- /autoplan-accepted:ceo -->

<!-- autoplan-accepted:design -->
- Record note: every requirement below is written into the plan sections above ("Try page frame", "Topics page as a course browser", "Stage details", the recipe, "Usage logging"). This list is the record of what the design review accepted.
- Try page frame (Wave 0): one `ModelFrame`, extracted from the existing physics-model layout. Header in reading order: course and unit label, concept title as the page's only heading (model label when no concept), a "try this" note of at most two lines, previous and next concept links with position. Fixed header height at 1280px so the page never scrolls; at 375px the stage is pinned at 45% of the screen while controls scroll beneath. The Saras mark links home. Tab title "<concept title> · Saras". Body text uses the body font variable; the warm tint and system-font fallback in `.viz` are removed.
- Navigation: previous and next follow catalog order within the unit and skip unmapped concepts; at a unit's last mapped concept next becomes the next unit's first mapped concept, or "Back to <course>". "All topics" returns to the same subject, search, toggle and open course. `Root` re-renders on hash change and the Try page re-resolves in place, so no full page reload between concepts.
- Controls kit (Wave 0): slider row, choice, number field with unit, readout row, legend, reset. It is the only source of controls; in-model cases use the choice control. No model picker and no preset picker. `scripts/check-models.test.ts` fails on a raw range or number input or a literal colour in a model folder. One number format through `fmt`: three significant figures, scientific notation below 0.001 and from 100,000, unit after a thin space, en dash for a missing value.
- Other Wave 0 kits: `Transport` (play, pause, scrub, speed) for every time-based model; `ExpressionInput` (debounced, keeps the last valid picture, names the offending token; presets with sliders first, typing second). Wave 1: `BoardStage` with `canvas` and `table` layouts, 12px minimum text, wide tables scrolling inside the board with the first column pinned; `Stepper` gains a caption line, a step counter and replay.
- Replaced from the CEO record: the stage no longer waits for the catalog when `c` is present (it renders from the path at once); search is across all subjects, not the active one; the two empty-state lines become four; a failed chunk offers a page reload, not an in-place retry; previous and position join the next link; pages no longer reload on a hash change.
- Stage states: loading (outline skeleton, fixed size, no shimmer under reduced motion), ready, chunk failed ("This model didn't load." with a page reload), refused ("Couldn't verify a correct visual for this" with the reason), crashed ("This model hit an error." with reset and a link to topics), no such model ("No model called X", X shortened to 40 characters). With `?c=` the model named by the path renders at once and swaps only if the concept maps elsewhere.
- Boundaries: controls clamp; a meaningful boundary is drawn and labelled (the nine listed in "Stage details"); only nonsense is refused, with the reason beside the control. A word-problem parse has three outcomes, each with its own line: filled, partial with the missing quantity named, not understood.
- Presets and notes: every preset is the default or mapped to a concept (registry test). A note has the form "Change X and watch Y: you should see Z". A concept maps to a preset that shows that concept at load, with its own note when the preset's does not name it; the second agent checks this.
- 3D: each 3D model has the one glass object and the 2D fallback named in "Stage details"; a fallback announces itself ("Showing the 2D view"); WebGL loss switches to it; on touch, orbiting is off until "Rotate" is tapped.
- Accessibility: every control keyboard-reachable, including stage draggables (arrow-key nudging and a mirrored number field); controls at least 44px tall at 375px; readouts in a polite live region; a one-sentence state description on the stage; under reduced motion time becomes a manual scrub and `ParticleBox` shows a still snapshot with a step button; nothing told apart by colour alone.
- Colour (before Wave 1): a six-step series ramp, a positive/negative pair, a reactant/product pair and element colours, defined once in `materials.ts` and the `.viz` variables; convention sets hue, the house palette sets lightness and saturation. Model CSS uses only existing variables.
- Topics page (Wave 0): interactive count on each tab; a concept with a model is the prominent chip, one without is a quiet chip that becomes "Noted" on click and logs `want`; search across all five subjects with per-tab match counts; "interactive only" switch, off by default; the four empty states in the plan; "N of M interactive" or a muted "none yet" per course; subject, query, toggle and open course kept in the address.
- Usage events: `open`, `interacted` (first control change), `engaged`, and `want`. A footer line on the Try and topics pages: "We count which models get opened. No names, no accounts."
- Verification: the second agent checks each model against the design checklist in the recipe; screenshots of each new model at 375px and 1280px, 3D and fallback, go to the founder with the PR.
- Deferred, to be written to TODOS.md on approval: a written design-token document.
<!-- /autoplan-accepted:design -->

<!-- autoplan-accepted:dx -->
- Record note: every requirement below is written into the plan sections above ("Engine shape", "Catalog and mapping", "The builder's path and the docs", "Messages a builder sees", "Try page frame", "Usage logging", "Continuous integration", the recipe, "Stage details"). This list is the record of what the DX review accepted.
- Contract (Wave 0): `src/engine/models/types.ts` defines `ModelMeta`, `Preset<S>`, `ValidationResult<S>` (with `field`), `ModelCore<S>`, `WidgetProps<S>` (`{ spec }`), `ConceptMap` (`mapped`, `declined`) and `ParseResult<S>` (`filled`, `partial`, `none`). A model folder has four required files, `meta.ts`, `core.ts`, `presets.ts` and `Widget.tsx`, each with one default export checked with `satisfies`; `concepts.ts`, `equations.ts`, `parse.ts`, `data.ts`, `widget.css` and the test file as the recipe requires. A widget owns its control state; `ModelFrame` supplies reset, the "interacted" event and the concept's note through context. A spec may hold arrays of flat records one level deep.
- Scaffold: `bun run new:model <type> [--stage] [--eq] [--wp] [--concepts]` writes a working folder (one-slider spec, default preset, `validate`, a kit-built widget, `concepts.ts` mapping the given ids to the default preset, a passing test), takes stage, Eq and WP from `planned.ts` when the type is listed, refuses a non-kebab-case type or an existing folder, marks the model `stub: true`, and prints the address and next commands. Check mode fails while any model is a stub. `scripts/new-model.test.ts` scaffolds one model per stage and asserts it registers and its test passes.
- Reference models: `function-grapher` (plot) and `projectile` (3D, equation set, parser) are whole in their folders; Wave 0 moves `projectile`'s maths, equations, parser and widget out of the shared files, leaving its schema branch and prompt text in `spec.ts`. The other six existing models are adapters with a `core.ts` that re-exports `validate`.
- Generated files: `meta.gen.ts` (with which optional files each folder has), `load.gen.ts` (dynamic imports of `core.ts` and `presets.ts`, lazy `Widget.tsx`), `concepts.gen.ts` (every `concepts.ts` and `presets.ts`; build and tests only) and `catalog/<subject>.json`, none committed. A folder registers only when all four required files exist by exact name; each run prints one line per folder. The generator runs on `postinstall` and before `dev`, `build`, `test`, `lint` and `typecheck` (a new script), and a Vite plugin re-runs it when a model folder gains or loses a file or a `meta.ts`, `presets.ts` or `concepts.ts` changes; a failed run in the dev server keeps the last good output.
- Catalog and build modes: the committed subject JSON no longer carries `specType`; the curriculum index helpers, the coverage page, the seed script and `src/topics/concepts.ts` read the mapping from the catalog or `concepts.gen.ts`. Normal mode: a pending exclusion for a now-mapped course is a warning, every other failure stops the build. Check mode (`bun run test:catalog`; CI and the integrator): that warning is an error, and the build also fails on a subject JSON that differs from what the raw files produce, on a stub, and on a remaining `KIT-REQUEST.md`. `validateMappings` is tested for both modes and for a concept both mapped and declined.
- Mapping: the integrator passes each model's concept ids to the scaffold on that model's branch; a builder may move a concept to `declined` with a reason (listed in the build's report) and does not add mappings; additions go through the integrator. One model per concept stays the rule.
- `planned.ts` lists every catalog model with `type`, `label`, `subject`, `stage`, `eq`, `wp` and `wave`; `specTypes.ts` shows built plus planned-and-not-built; `registry.test.ts` asserts stage, and the presence of `equations.ts` and `parse.ts` exactly when the list says Eq and WP.
- Tests: `bun run test` runs every `src/**/*.test.ts` and `scripts/**/*.test.ts`, prints a line per file and a summary, and exits non-zero if any failed; `bun run test <text>` filters by path; `src/engine/testkit.ts` provides `check` and `near` (expected and actual printed); a `bunfig.toml` preload makes `bun test` print "Use `bun run test`" and exit.
- Worktree: `bun run setup:worktree` installs, copies `.env.local` from the main checkout when present without overwriting one, picks and saves a free port, runs the generator and prints the address; `vite.config.ts` reads the saved port and is strict about it; `src/lib/supabase.ts` does not throw when the environment values are missing. `scripts/setup-worktree.test.ts` covers the port and the no-overwrite rule. Target: fresh worktree to the stub on screen in under five minutes.
- Sweep: `#/sweep` and `#/sweep/<type>` (development only) record per preset whether it mounted and drew, with the error text, on the page and on `window.__sweep`. `bun run sweep [type] [--width 375|1280] [--nowebgl]` uses Playwright (a new development dependency), starts the dev server if needed, prints the table, saves screenshots to `sweep-shots/` (not committed) and exits non-zero on failure; with no flags it runs all four combinations. Route options sit inside the hash after `?`; `nowebgl=1` also works on `#/try/...`.
- Second stage: `meta.ts` may declare `aux`. At 1280px the stage column splits with the aux stage below at a third of its height; at 375px a two-button switch on the pinned stage chooses which is pinned and the other sits first in the scrolling column. `unit-circle`, `kinematics-1d`, `energy-conservation`, `ideal-gas`, `states-of-matter` and `conformations` declare one.
- Number format: `fmt(value, options)`; three significant figures by default, integers below 100,000 in full, `sig`, `kind: 'int'` and `kind: 'percent'` options, and readout rows that accept an exact string. The eight existing models move to it and their readouts are checked by hand in Wave 0.
- Usage logging: nothing is recorded from a development build, from `#/sweep` or `#/kits`, or with `localStorage.saras_no_usage` set; events go to the console there. Rows carry the app version. `supabase/model_events.sql` can be run again safely. The coverage page shows one of five states (on, table missing, rejected, unreachable, not configured), learned from a probe row the table's checks refuse, so the probe writes nothing. Tests cover the no-send cases and the five outcomes.
- Development warnings: every silent fallback (unknown preset, unknown or unmapped `c`, a crashed model) writes one console warning in a development build with what was asked for and what was shown.
- CI: the workflow adds the 2D sweep; Bun is pinned in `package.json` and read from there; `package-lock.json` is deleted; Wave 0's PR confirms the Vercel preview builds with the generator; a refused workflow push leaves `docs/ci.yml.txt` and a TODOS.md entry.
- Docs (Wave 0): `docs/MODELS.md` (glossary, the five steps, a skeleton per file, the two reference models walked through, a command table, the kit-request and declined-concept rules, an entry per message, a dated "Changes" list), `docs/MODEL-REVIEW.md` (checklist and fixed pass/fail report), `docs/MODEL-BRIEF.md` (the per-builder brief and what the hand-off report must contain, including what slowed the builder down); `README.md`, `CLAUDE.md` and `src/data/curriculum/README.md` corrected, with counts copied from the build's report.
- Kits: doc comments on every kit's props; a development-only `#/kits` page; an overlay slot in every stage; `KIT-REQUEST.md` answered by the integrator between sub-PRs; `safeExpression` returns a function or a failure with message, token and position; `units.ts` gains `toKelvin` and `fromKelvin`, with kelvin shown where the equation needs absolute temperature and degrees Celsius elsewhere. The contract and the kits change only through the integrator; a breaking kit change lands with its model edits in one PR.
- Messages: the 21 messages in "Messages a builder sees", each stating the problem with the actual value, the file and id, and the fix, each asserted by the test of the check that produces it.
- Model rules: `scripts/check-models.test.ts` fails on a raw `input` element or a literal colour in a model folder, allows `none`, `transparent`, `currentColor` and `inherit`, honours an `allow-raw: <reason>` comment and prints the number of exemptions; the raw-input rule also shows as an ESLint warning.
- Measurement: after Wave 1 the integrator records the time from a fresh worktree to the stub on screen and the builders' "what slowed me down" lines, and fixes the guide or the scaffold before Wave 2.
- Replaced from the earlier records: `manifest.ts` becomes `core.ts` (with `validate`) and `presets.ts`, and the registry test that compared preset ids in `meta.ts` and `manifest.ts` is dropped; the catalog is generated, not committed, and the integrator no longer regenerates committed files at merge; subject JSON is no longer stamped with `specType`; the test runner reports every failure instead of stopping at the first; the integrator scaffolds each model's folder on its own branch instead of writing `concepts.ts` ahead of fan-out; `fmt` shows integers in full and takes options; usage logging is silent in development rather than warning once, and the coverage page has five states instead of two; `?nowebgl` becomes `nowebgl=1` inside the hash; `projectile` is no longer left as an adapter.
- Effort: Wave 0 becomes about 9 to 12 hours of agent time (about 4 weeks for a human team).
- Deferred, to be written to TODOS.md on approval: the WebGL sweep in CI; a custom ESLint plugin for the model rules; more than one model per concept in the catalog; moving the other six existing models' internals into their folders (replaces the CEO record's "the 8 legacy models").
<!-- /autoplan-accepted:dx -->

<!-- autoplan-accepted:eng -->
- Record note: every requirement below is written into the plan sections above ("Wave 0 in four PRs", "Engine shape", "Try page frame", "Usage logging", "Continuous integration", "Catalog and mapping", "Deep links", "The builder's path and the docs", "Messages a builder sees", the recipe, "Stage details", "Order and delivery"). This list is the record of what the engineering review accepted.
- Delivery: Wave 0 lands as four stacked PRs. 0a (0.3.4.0): the test runner and `testkit.ts`, CI, one lockfile and a pinned Bun, tests that pin today's behaviour of `projectile`, `wave-oscillator`, `free-body-diagram`, `circular-motion` and `function-grapher` (closed forms and the exact readout text of each default preset) written against the current code, the topics column fix, and the spec endpoint switched off. 0b (0.3.5.0): model folders, the contract, the generator and plugin, the registry, the catalog and single mapping source, the gate, `planned.ts`, `projectile` and `function-grapher` moved in whole, with no change in behaviour. 0c (0.3.6.0): `ModelFrame` and slots, the Wave 0 kits, `fmt`, in-place navigation with a route error boundary, deep links, the topics page on the catalog, the six legacy widgets slot-wrapped. 0d (0.3.7.0): usage logging and SQL, the sweep and browser specs, the scaffold, worktree setup, the three docs, the Algebra 1 mappings. Waves 1 to 6 take versions from 0.3.8.0; 15 PRs in all. Estimates are replaced after 0b and after Wave 1.
- Regression contract: the 0a tests pass unchanged through 0b; the `fmt` change in 0c updates the pinned readout strings in the same PR.
- Spec endpoint: `api/spec.ts` answers "not found" unless `SPEC_API_ENABLED` is set and returns generic errors; a rate limit is a condition of enabling it.
- Contract: `ModelCore<S, D>` has `ranges`, `validate` and `derive`; `WidgetProps<S>` is `{ spec, set }`; `equations.ts` exports `ModelEquations<S>` (`set`, `fields`). The frame owns the live spec, runs `validate` on every change and shows a refusal by the control bound to its `field`; controls take ranges from `core.ranges`; a widget keeps only non-model state; a widget renders inside `Frame.Stage`, `Frame.Aux`, `Frame.Controls` and `Frame.Readouts`; the frame renders the equation panel (feeding `useEquationPanel` from the live spec, substituting the solved value, locking that control) and the word-problem bar (applying `patch` with `set`); anything an equation solves for is a spec field; the widget is keyed on type and preset; the first input event is "interacted". The stage and readouts draw `derive`'s numbers. Symbolic-against-numeric comparisons are test-time only.
- Legacy six (`legacy: true`): slot-wrapped in 0c, keeping their own state, `{ spec }` props, their own equation panel and word-problem bar; exempt from `ranges`, `derive` and range sampling. `projectile` and `function-grapher` take the full contract.
- Generator: `scripts/gen.ts` (TypeScript, Bun) writes `meta.gen.ts`, `load.gen.ts` (one loader per model), `pure.gen.ts` (every `meta`, `core`, `presets`, `concepts`; build and tests only) and `catalog.gen.json`, each renamed into place and only when changed. The Vite plugin runs it at dev-server and build start and on model-folder changes, and ignores its own outputs; hooks remain for `postinstall`, `test`, `lint` and `typecheck`. A production deploy runs check mode. Stubs are left out of every production build. `tsconfig.scripts.json` typechecks `scripts/`, the curriculum build and `api/`.
- Catalog: one file for all subjects; character-code sorting; `validateMappings` also fails on a course missing from or unknown to `courseOrder.ts`; a model with no mapped concept is a warning in normal mode and an error in check mode.
- Model rules: ESLint errors for a model's pure files importing React, three or CSS, for a model importing another model, `supabase`, `usage` or `Label3D`, for `fetch`, `localStorage`, `location` or `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` in a model, and for a raw `input`; `check-models` covers literal colours in CSS and TSX; `bun run check:branch <type>` fails a model branch that changed a file outside its folder; `bun run check:bundle` fails when a plot or board model loads three, a model without a typed expression loads mathjs, or the Try page's first load exceeds the 0c figure by more than a tenth.
- Tests: range sampling in `registry.test.ts` (each control at both ends and at seeded points, every preset as base: `validate` passes and every `derive` number is finite or an explicit "undefined here"); the runner fails a file with no assertion, over 30 seconds, or with a rejected promise; a schema-drift test for the legacy branches in `spec.ts` and a check that `api/spec.ts`'s imports stay free of React and CSS; `scripts/gen.test.ts` covers write-on-change.
- Browser specs (`bun run test:e2e`, Playwright, in CI against the built site): the frame at both widths; the six stage states through a fixture model; every deep-link row; topics filters and empty states; the in-place walk, including leaving the sign-in and onboarding pages; a tab-order walk per stage kind; reduced motion; an accessibility scan of each model's default preset (axe).
- Sweep: `#/sweep/<type>` in development builds and builds made with `VITE_SWEEP=1`; one model per page; records mount, which renderer drew, that it drew, and a scan for `NaN`, `undefined`, `Infinity` and `[object Object]`; fails on an unexpected fallback; `--nowebgl` disables WebGL in the browser; CI runs it against `vite build` then `vite preview`; locally it checks the dev server is this worktree's own.
- Routing: a route error boundary; decode failures treated as an unknown type; `#/try?c=<id>` with no type shows the skeleton until the catalog loads; focus moves to the heading and the title is announced after an in-place move; `open` is sent once, after the address settles.
- Frame details: the pinned stage uses the small viewport height with a floor and is unpinned under about 480px of visible height; readouts are announced after a control settles and not while playing; `fmt` keeps three significant figures and never fewer digits than the whole-number part, with fixed-width numerals; the three private copies of `fmt` are deleted; WebGL is probed once, an unmount's context loss is ignored, a real loss waits for one restore.
- Kits: no barrel file; `DragHandle`; each kit built against its named hardest consumers as fixtures on `#/kits` before its wave's fan-out; `ParticleBox` as core, canvas renderer and three renderer in separate files; `safeExpression` with its own mathjs instance, a node allowlist, declared symbols only, non-number results as "undefined here", and three profiles; `function-grapher` rebuilt on it and the old check deleted; a shared quantity reader, a 500-character cap and a hostile-input time test for parsers; KaTeX through one kit component with trust off; concentration stored in moles per litre.
- Usage logging: sent with plain `fetch` and the public key only; `engaged` is a delta sent on hide and on moving to another model; `supabase/model_events.sql` creates the table if missing and drops and recreates its rules (event one of four names, shaped type, preset and concept, seconds 0 to 1800, UUID session, database-set time, insert only); the probe uses a named event rule; the status shows only in development or with the founder's flag; the storage read is guarded; the sign-in provider skips its session calls without configuration; `bun scripts/usage-smoke.ts` is run by the founder signed out and signed in; the report counts only known model types and carries the statement that deletes rows older than 180 days.
- CI: typecheck, test, `test:catalog`, lint, build, `check:bundle`, browser specs and the 2D sweep; read-only permissions, pinned actions, cached browser.
- Worktree setup copies only `VITE_` lines of `.env.local`, derives the port from the worktree's path and checks it is free; the dev server answers an identity request.
- Catalog corrections: `surface-3d` leaves undefined cells as holes; `free-body-diagram` refuses a static coefficient below the kinetic one; `titration` and `equilibrium` solve by bounded bisection to a stated tolerance; `stereochemistry` stores priorities for its curated set and tests them against the atomic-number rule; `conic-sections` tilts by slider; the nine edges in "Stage details" behave as listed.
- Review: the second agent, not the builder, removes `stub` on a pass; `data.ts` sources are addresses that can be opened.
- Messages: the table gains five rows (nothing mapped, range sampling, imports, branch scope, a test with no checks) and a bundle row, 27 in all.
- Replaced from the earlier records: Wave 0 is four PRs, not one, and the total is 15 PRs, not 11; versions for Waves 1 to 6 start at 0.3.8.0, not 0.3.5.0; `WidgetProps` is `{ spec, set }` and the frame owns the spec, not the widget; `ModelCore` gains `ranges` and `derive`; `scripts/gen-models.mjs` is `scripts/gen.ts`; `concepts.gen.ts` is `pure.gen.ts`; three lazy imports per model become one loader; the per-subject catalog files become one `catalog.gen.json`, so the subject-prefix lookup and per-subject failure states go; `#/sweep` no longer mounts every model on one page and CI sweeps the built site; `fmt` no longer shows "integers below 100,000 in full" by default; `planned.ts` uses `subjects`; the builder no longer removes `stub`; the raw-input rule is an ESLint error, not a warning; setup no longer copies the whole `.env.local`; `#/try?c=` with no type no longer shows `projectile` first; "the 21 messages" are 27; Wave 0's effort is about 20 to 26 hours and everything about 115 to 165.
- Deferred, written to TODOS.md: a rate limit on `api/spec.ts`; converting the six legacy widgets to the full contract; sweeping only changed models in CI; a scheduled clean-up of `model_events`.
