History: the CEO phase's accepted list from the first run, kept verbatim. The current list is in the re-run section below.

````text
<!-- autoplan-accepted:ceo -->
- Record note: every requirement below is already written into the plan sections above. This list is the record of what the CEO review accepted, kept for later phases.
- Scope: 65 new models in six waves of 7, 11, 20, 10, 11 and 6, plus five extensions to existing models; 73 models in total, delivered in 11 PRs of at most seven new models each (Wave 0 alone, then 1, 2, 3, 2, 2, 1 PRs for Waves 1 to 6), with the estimates replaced by Wave 1's actuals before Wave 2 starts. One primary interaction per model; variations are presets. `rc-circuit` is a separate Wave 3 model whose tests assert V0 * (1 - exp(-t / RC)) for charging, V0 * exp(-t / RC) for discharging, and a time constant of R * C; the circuit builder's steady-state solver is not extended. `linear-transform` is 2x2. `vector-field` is a 2D field with 2D curl and divergence.
- Goal: one gate and one report. The gate, applied by the curriculum build (`bun run gen:curriculum`): every course has a mapped concept or an entry in `exclusions.ts`; Wave 0 seeds one "pending Wave N" entry per uncovered course following the course-to-wave table in the plan, each wave deletes the entries it satisfies, and after Wave 6 only permanent exclusions with reasons may remain ("Computer programming" is the one known permanent exclusion); an entry for an already-mapped course or an unknown course also fails the gate. The report: per-course coverage and unmapped high 3D-fit concepts (230 are marked high-fit). Expected coverage of roughly 250 to 300 of 734 concepts is a report, not a target.
- Recipe: a TypeScript spec interface with named presets, shaped as a flat record of numbers, strings, enums and arrays of those; a one-sentence "try this" prompt in every preset's `notes`, shown on the Try page; LaTeX strings are code constants, never built from typed text; reference data in a `data.ts` with a cited source per table; a second agent that did not build the model repeats the hands-on pass and checks reference data against its source; one deterministic core shared by gate and renderer; an equation set and a word-problem parser exactly where the catalog's "Eq / WP" column says yes (a parser without an equation set only fills the widget's inputs); labels in demand-frameloop 3D scenes are HTML over the canvas or a legend beside it, never `Label3D`; one of three stages (3D, plot, board); keyboard-reachable controls; usable at 375px; one primary interaction; a test file with no node or bun APIs that includes "every preset validates"; verification by build, lint, the sweep page at 375px and 1280px with and without WebGL, and a hands-on pass.
- Engine shape (Wave 0): one folder per model with `meta.ts` (light, pure data), `manifest.ts` (presets and `validate`, pure), `core.ts`, optional `equations.ts` and `parse.ts`, `Widget.tsx`, `widget.css`, `concepts.ts` and a test file. `scripts/gen-models.mjs` (plain Node) writes `meta.gen.ts`, `load.gen.ts` and `concepts.gen.ts`, gitignored and produced by predev, prebuild, pretest and `gen:curriculum`; it registers a folder only when `meta.ts`, `manifest.ts` and `Widget.tsx` all exist, so a folder holding only a pre-assigned `concepts.ts` is ignored. `specTypes.ts` is derived from `meta.gen.ts` plus a static `PLANNED` list. `bun run test` runs every `src/**/*.test.ts`; `bun run test:catalog` runs the curriculum build in check mode and is run by the integrator on the combined tree, not by model branches. The existing 8 models get adapter folders (their example specs move from `VisualizePanel.tsx` into the manifests as presets), keep their internals and their two existing test files, and the five without tests (`projectile`, `wave-oscillator`, `free-body-diagram`, `circular-motion`, `function-grapher`) gain closed-form test files. Kits live in `src/engine/kits/` and are built by the integrator before the wave that first needs them (`PlotStage` Wave 0, exposing its scales, a pointer-to-data mapping and an overlay slot; `Stepper` Wave 1; `ParticleBox` Wave 3 as a seeded core with SVG and three renderers), together with any unit families the wave needs in `units.ts` (temperature stays in kelvin). The Try page has no model or preset picker. The Try page shows a stage-shaped skeleton while a model loads and a "This model didn't load" notice with retry when a chunk fails. A widget that throws shows "This model hit an error" with a link to topics. The tab title is the concept title or model label. A development-only `#/sweep` page mounts every model and preset inside an error boundary, with `?nowebgl` forcing the fallback. `safeExpression` in the kits is the only path from typed text to a function (mathjs parse, allowlisted nodes and function names, 200-character cap). `Stepper` stops at the last step, pauses on scrub and has no autoplay under reduced motion; `ParticleBox` caps at 400 particles; each kit's pure part has a unit test.
- Usage logging (Wave 0): `src/lib/usage.ts` records `open` and `engaged` (visible seconds, capped at 30 minutes, sent with keepalive on page hide) with model type, preset, concept id and a random per-tab session id, into a Supabase table `model_events` defined with insert-only row-level security in `supabase/model_events.sql`, which the founder runs once; a failed insert is dropped, warns once in development, and the coverage page shows "usage logging: on" or "table missing"; `supabase/usage_report.sql` reports opens and returning sessions per course per week. Tests: payload builder caps and drops; a rejected insert does not throw.
- Continuous integration (Wave 0): `.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs install, `bun run test`, `bun run test:catalog`, lint and build on pull requests; if the push is refused for lack of workflow permission the file is handed to the founder instead.
- Registry tests: `validateMappings` is a pure function tested on fixtures for each failure (unknown concept, duplicate mapping, missing preset, raw `specType`, uncovered course, stale or unknown exclusion); the generator's complete-folder rule is tested in `scripts/gen-models.test.ts`; meta types are unique and match their folder; preset ids in `meta.ts` and `manifest.ts` agree; every preset validates; the default preset exists; every mapped concept id exists; no concept maps to two models; every mapped preset exists.
- Mapping (Wave 0): a model's `concepts.ts` is the only place a concept is mapped. The 4 existing mappings move there; `specType` is removed from the raw curriculum files and the build fails if a raw file sets it. `bun run gen:curriculum` writes one committed catalog file per subject: courses in the order of `courseOrder.ts`, units in each course's `units` order, concepts in raw file order, each as id, title and optional `[type, preset]`; units with no concepts are omitted. Before each wave's fan-out the integrator writes each new model's `concepts.ts`; when two models could claim a concept it goes to the one whose primary interaction is that concept.
- Deep links (Wave 0): the resolution table in the plan, with `#/try` pinned to `projectile`, `?c=<conceptId>` winning over the path, unknown or unmapped ids ignored, an unknown preset falling back to the type's default, and an unknown type showing a "No model called X" notice with nothing rendered in its place. Header data comes from the lazily loaded subject catalog: with `c` present the stage shows its skeleton until the catalog resolves, and a failed load or an unknown subject prefix makes `c` unknown. The next link follows catalog order and skips unmapped concepts. Tests: a unit test of the resolver with one case per table row, and of the next-in-unit lookup (middle, last in unit, unknown id).
- Topics page (Wave 0): reads the generated catalog instead of Supabase; five subject tabs; search by case-insensitive title substring; an "interactive only" toggle; AND semantics; while either is active, units and courses with no match are hidden and courses with a match are expanded; "No concepts match" when the query is non-empty and nothing matches, "No interactive models in this subject yet" when the query is empty, the toggle is on and nothing matches; "N of M interactive" per course, counted unfiltered; the existing error line with a retry button when a catalog fails to load; Try links of the form `#/try/<type>/<preset>?c=<conceptId>`. Tests: unit tests of the pure filter and count functions (match, no match, empty query, mixed case, toggle alone, toggle with search), and a browser check that walks two next hops through "Forms of linear equations".
- `function-grapher` (Wave 0) is brought to the full recipe on `PlotStage` with presets for the eleven Algebra 1 concepts named in the plan and a test that every preset passes `validateFunctionGrapher` and that these eleven are finite at every sample across their domain.
- Existing-model extensions (Wave 1), each with assertions in that model's tests, new spec fields optional with defaults so `src/engine/spec.ts` schema and prompt text and `api/spec.ts` stay untouched: `circular-motion` gains mass and F = m v^2 / r; `wave-oscillator` gains F = k x; `circuit-diagram` gains named network presets and a test that Kirchhoff's current and voltage laws hold in the solver's result; `free-body-diagram` gains an optional static coefficient (defaulting to the existing `friction`, which stays kinetic), an optional applied force F and a flat-surface preset, holding while abs(m g sin(theta) - F) is at most mu_s m g cos(theta) and otherwise sliding with kinetic friction opposing the motion; `orbit-sim` gains an orbiting mass with F = M m / r^2 and U = -M m / r in natural units. One agent does the five in sequence.
- The coverage dashboard (`#/coverage`) uses the real mapping as the base layer; a real mapping wins and is read-only, and the localStorage planning layer applies only to unmapped concepts.
- Delivery: Wave 0 is version 0.3.4.0 and ships alone; each later PR (at most seven new models) is one version; new-model branches touch only their own folder and run their own test file, `registry.test.ts`, build, lint and the sweep for their model; the integrator merges, deletes satisfied exclusion entries, runs `bun run gen:curriculum`, then `bun run test`, `bun run test:catalog`, build, lint and the full sweep on the combined tree.
- On approval a dated note is added to `docs/DESIGN.md` recording the founder's decision to build the prebuilt library past the original ~12 types. The plan carries an "Alternatives considered" section.
- Deferred, to be written to TODOS.md when the plan is approved: path routes with per-concept titles and prerendering; a free-text entry point on the Try page with the classifier, including schema branches and prompt paragraphs for the new models; shareable model state in the URL; multi-step guided challenges per preset; migrating the 8 legacy models' internals into their folders; crash reporting from students' browsers; 3x3 matrices in `linear-transform`; 3D fields, 3D curl, line integrals and conservative fields in `vector-field`.
<!-- /autoplan-accepted:ceo -->
````

#### Section 1: Architecture

```
            BUILD TIME                                      RUN TIME
 raw/*.json ───────────┐                        #/topics ─► TopicsPage ──lazy──┐
 models/*/concepts.ts ─┼─► build.ts ─► <subject>.json                          ▼
   (via concepts.gen)  │      │        catalog/<subject>.json ◄───────── catalog chunk
 models/*/meta.ts ─────┘      └─ validateMappings: gate + report                ▲
   (via meta.gen) ─────────────────────────► specTypes.ts, #/coverage           │
        ▲                                                                       │
 scripts/gen-models.mjs (predev, prebuild, pretest, gen:curriculum)             │
                                                                                │
 #/try/<type>/<preset>?c=<id> ─► resolve (meta.gen + catalog when c present) ───┘
        │ unknown type ─► "No model called X"
        ▼
 load.gen: import(manifest) + lazy(Widget) ──fail──► "This model didn't load" [retry]
        ▼
 manifest.validate(preset) ──invalid──► "Couldn't verify a correct visual"
        ▼
 <ErrorBoundary> Widget on Scene3D | PlotStage | board ──throws──► "This model hit an error"
        │                 └─ no WebGL ─► 2D SVG fallback from the same core
        ▼
 usage.ts ─► Supabase model_events (insert only; failure dropped)
```

Try page state machine: `resolving` to `not-found`, or to `loading`; `loading` to
`load-failed` (retry returns to `loading`), to `invalid`, or to `ready`; `ready` to
`crashed`. There is no path from `not-found`, `invalid` or `crashed` to a rendered model
other than a new URL, which is what "never a guessed visual" requires.

Findings:
- WARNING, resolved (CEO-39): the plan had no rescue for a widget that throws while
  rendering; React would unmount the page. An error boundary is now in Wave 0.
- OK: coupling. New coupling is one-directional: pages depend on generated indexes; models
  depend on kits and shared engine helpers; nothing depends on a specific model. The
  curriculum build depends on model `meta.ts` and `concepts.ts` only through generated
  pure files, so it never imports React or three.
- OK: scaling. The first thing to strain at 10x models is the eager meta index (about 300
  bytes a model, so about 22 KB at 73 and 220 KB at 730), which is the point to paginate
  it by subject. Nothing here is on a server path.
- OK: single points of failure. Supabase leaves the read path for topics; it remains for
  sign-in and for usage logging, where failure is dropped.
- OK: rollback. Every wave is additive and ships as its own PR; reverting the PR (or a
  Vercel instant rollback) restores the previous state in minutes. Wave 0's topics data
  source change reverts with its PR.
- Platform note: the registry plus the catalog are what later features key off (a
  classifier that picks a type, per-concept pages, the analogy and walkthrough channels).

#### Section 2: Error and rescue map

| Codepath | What can go wrong | Named outcome | Rescued | User sees |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| deep-link resolver | unknown type | NotFound | yes | "No model called X" with a link to topics |
| deep-link resolver | unknown preset | default preset | yes | the model with its label |
| catalog import on Try (`?c=`) | chunk fails; unknown subject prefix | concept ignored | yes | the model resolved from the path |
| `load.gen` manifest or widget import | chunk load error | LoadFailed | yes | "This model didn't load" with retry |
| `manifest.validate(preset)` | spec refused by the gate | Invalid | yes | "Couldn't verify a correct visual" (existing copy) |
| widget render | exception | Crashed | yes (new) | "This model hit an error" with a link to topics |
| `safeExpression` | parse error; disallowed node; over 200 characters | reason string | yes | inline message under the input; the picture keeps the last valid function |
| `solveFor` (existing) | no solution | SolveResult with reason | yes | "no solution" hint in the equation panel |
| word-problem parser | no numbers found | empty `found` | yes | "Couldn't find the numbers" (existing copy) |
| topics catalog import | chunk fails | error state | yes | error line with retry |
| `usage.logModelEvent` | table missing; offline; rejected | dropped | yes, by design | nothing; one development console warning; status on `#/coverage` |
| `build.ts` / `validateMappings` | unknown concept; duplicate mapping; missing preset; raw `specType`; uncovered course; stale or unknown exclusion | non-zero exit listing every failure | n/a (build time) | the integrator's command fails |
| `gen-models.mjs` | incomplete folder | skipped and listed in output | yes | n/a |
| `hasWebGL()` false | no WebGL | 2D fallback | yes | the SVG version |

No catch-all handlers are introduced: each row names its outcome. Gaps after CEO-39: 0.

#### Section 3: Security and threat model

| Threat | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigated |
|---|---|---|---|
| Typed expression reaching mathjs `evaluate` with unsafe functions | low | medium | yes: `safeExpression` allowlist, length cap, no raw evaluate (CEO-40) |
| HTML injection through KaTeX output (`dangerouslySetInnerHTML` in the equation panel) | low | medium | yes: LaTeX strings are code constants (CEO-40) |
| Path segment or `?c=` reflected in the not-found notice | low | low | yes: rendered as React text, never as HTML |
| Spam inserts into `model_events` with the public key | medium | low | yes: insert-only row-level security, length and range checks, no select; nothing user-facing reads the table |
| New dependency risk | n/a | n/a | none added; the generator is dependency-free |
| Secrets | n/a | n/a | none added; the workflow uses no secrets |

No per-user data is read or written by anything in this plan, so there is no authorization
or object-reference surface. Usage rows carry no account id and no free text.

#### Section 4: Data flow and interaction edge cases

```
hash ─► parse ─► resolve ─► load chunks ─► validate ─► render ─► log
 │nil     │odd     │unknown   │fail          │refused    │throws    │fail
 ▼        ▼        ▼          ▼              ▼           ▼          ▼
 default  ignored  notice or  retry notice   refusal     crash      dropped
 model    parts    default                   notice      notice
```

| Interaction | Edge case | Handled | How |
|---|---|---|---|
| next-in-unit link | double click; back button | yes | every hash change reloads the page; the resolver is a pure function of the URL |
| topics search | fast typing over 438 math concepts | yes | pure in-memory filter, no request |
| interactive-only toggle | subject with nothing mapped | yes | its own empty-state line |
| equation panel inputs | non-numeric or out-of-range value | yes | existing panel handling; new models reuse the panel |
| `Stepper` | play at the last step; scrub while playing; reduced motion | yes (CEO-41) | stops; pauses; no autoplay |
| `ParticleBox` | zero or very large particle count | yes (CEO-41) | capped at 400; readouts are closed-form either way |
| animated model in a hidden tab | engaged time inflating | yes | only visible seconds are counted |
| sweep page | many 3D scenes in a row | yes | one model mounted at a time, unmounted before the next |
| slow connection | model chunk arriving late | yes | stage-shaped skeleton |

Async ordering: the only shared mutable state across awaits is the Try page's resolution
when `?c=` is present (catalog import against manifest import). The invariant is "the
concept wins over the path". Both completion orders are safe because the resolver runs
once, after the catalog promise settles, and only then starts the manifest import; the
resolver unit test covers the settled-with-failure order. Unhandled edge cases: 0.

#### Section 5: Code quality

- OK: organisation. One folder per model with fixed file names is easier to read than
  five 500-line shared files; kits hold what is shared.
- WARNING, accepted as debt: two layouts will coexist (the 8 legacy models keep their
  maths in `validate.ts`, `equations.ts`, `wordProblems.ts`). Moving them is a TODO, not
  part of this plan, because it is a rewrite of working code with no user-visible gain.
- OK: DRY. `PlotStage`, `Stepper`, `ParticleBox` and `safeExpression` each replace what
  would otherwise be copied into 10 to 25 models.
- OK: over-engineering check. The generator and three generated files exist because 73
  hand-maintained import lists is the failure this plan diagnoses; nothing is abstracted
  ahead of a second consumer (kits are built in the wave that first needs them).
- No new function in the plan is specified with more than five branches; the resolver is
  the densest and is a table.

#### Section 6: Tests

```
NEW CODEPATH                         TEST                                   TYPE
gen-models.mjs complete-folder rule  scripts/gen-models.test.ts             unit
validateMappings (6 failure kinds)   fixtures                               unit
registry invariants                  src/engine/models/registry.test.ts     unit
catalog staleness                    bun run test:catalog                   integration
deep-link resolver (7 rows)          one case per row                       unit
next-in-unit lookup                  middle, last, unknown                  unit
topics filter and counts             6 cases                                unit
usage payload and failure            caps, drops, rejected insert           unit
safeExpression                       allowed, refused, too long             unit
kit cores (scales, reducer, sim)     per kit                                unit
each model core, gate, equations,
  parser, presets                    <type>.test.ts                         unit
each widget, both stages             #/sweep at 375 and 1280, with and
                                     without WebGL                          system (scripted)
each model's behaviour               hands-on pass by a second agent        system (manual)
chunk failure, error boundary        blocked chunk; forced throw            manual
```

- The test that makes shipping at 2am safe: `registry.test.ts` plus `test:catalog` plus a
  clean sweep. The hostile test: odd strings into the resolver and `safeExpression`. The
  chaos test: block a model chunk and take Supabase away; the page must stay usable.
- Flakiness: simulations are seeded; the one network-dependent test on this branch (the
  live-table column check) is removed with the query it guarded.
- WARNING, accepted: there is no component or browser test framework in the repo, so
  widgets are verified by the sweep page and by hand. Adding one is new infrastructure and
  is left for the Eng phase to weigh.
- No prompt or LLM code changes in this plan, so no eval suite applies.

#### Section 7: Performance

- Eager payload on the Try page: the meta index (about 22 KB at 73 models) plus one
  model's chunks. Topics loads one subject's catalog (math is the largest, about 45 KB).
- Slowest new paths: regenerating a `surface-3d` mesh while a slider drags; the
  `vector-field` arrow grid; `ParticleBox` stepping. The particle cap is set (400); mesh
  and grid budgets are referred to the Eng phase as per-model limits.
- One WebGL scene is mounted at a time, including on the sweep page.
- No database queries, jobs or connection pools are added to the request path.

#### Section 8: Observability

- The signal that it is working: `model_events` rows, read through
  `supabase/usage_report.sql` (opens and returning sessions per course per week).
- The signal that it is broken: the coverage page's "usage logging: table missing", the
  build's coverage report, and the sweep page's per-preset result.
- GAP, accepted: there is no error reporting from students' browsers (a crash shows the
  boundary notice but tells the founder nothing). A crash event could reuse `model_events`
  later; left as a TODO.

#### Section 9: Deployment and rollout

- Vercel builds on merge. The generated registry files are gitignored, so the build
  depends on `prebuild` running there; the PR's preview deployment is the check, and it is
  part of the Wave 0 verification before merge.
- No migrations run automatically. The one schema change (`model_events`) is a file the
  founder applies by hand, and the code tolerates its absence.
- Rollback: revert the PR or use Vercel's instant rollback. Static site, so there is no
  old-and-new-code window.
- Post-deploy check, first five minutes: `#/topics` shows five tabs with counts, a "Try"
  link opens its model, the next link works, the console is clean, `#/coverage` shows the
  logging status.
- Risk flagged: 1 (the preview build must be confirmed to run `prebuild`).

#### Section 10: Long-term trajectory

- Reversibility: 4 of 5. Everything is additive; the topics data source and the test
  runner are the only replacements and both revert cleanly.
- Debt items: 3 (two code layouts; hash routing; no component tests).
- Path dependency: the `(type, preset)` mapping and the per-subject catalog become the
  spine other features attach to, which is the intent.
- The one-year question: a new engineer reads one model folder and the README the DX phase
  will ask for, and can add a model without touching shared files.
- Cherry-pick retrospective: instrumentation and CI were first deferred and then accepted
  once the independent voice made the same case; nothing rejected turned out to be
  load-bearing.

#### Section 11: Design and UX

```
#/topics                                    #/try/<type>/<preset>?c=<id>
┌ tabs: Physics Math Biology Chem Comp ┐    ┌ Concept title            all topics ┐
│ [search........] [interactive only]  │    │ Course · Unit            Next: ... →│
│ ▸ Algebra 1          11 of 45        │    │ "try this" note                     │
│   Forms of linear equations          │ ─► │ ┌ stage ────────┐ ┌ equation panel ┐│
│   [Point-slope Try→] [Slope-int Try→]│    │ │ 3D|plot|board │ │ (when Eq yes)  ││
│ ▸ Algebra 2          0 of 56         │    │ └───────────────┘ └────────────────┘│
└──────────────────────────────────────┘    └─────────────────────────────────────┘
```

| Feature | Loading | Empty | Error | Success | Partial |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Topics subject panel | skeleton courses (exists) | two empty-state lines | error line with retry | accordions with counts | filter active: only matching units |
| Try page model | stage-shaped skeleton | n/a | not-found, didn't load, couldn't verify, hit an error | model on its stage | no WebGL: 2D fallback |
| Try page header | model label until the catalog resolves | no next link at the end of a unit | concept ignored | title, course, unit, next | n/a |

- OK: the plan stays inside the existing design system (obsidian, pearl, glass, azure) and
  names it in the recipe; it specifies 375px, keyboard reach and reduced motion.
- WARNING, referred to the Design phase: the `board` stage has no visual definition
  beyond "flat SVG board in the same palette", and 19 models use it; the skeleton shapes,
  the header layout and whether "interactive only" starts on are also theirs to settle.
- Recommendation: run the design review on this plan (it runs next in this pipeline), and
  `/design-review` on the live site after Wave 0.

#### NOT in scope (CEO phase)

Deferred, each with its answer:
- Path routes, per-concept titles and prerendering (CEO-36: app-wide routing change).
- Free-text entry and the classifier, with schema and prompt for new models (CEO-14).
- Shareable model state in the URL (CEO-7).
- Multi-step guided challenges (CEO-8, narrowed).
- Migrating the 8 legacy models' internals into their folders (Section 5 debt).
- Crash reporting from students' browsers (Section 8).
- 3x3 matrices; 3D fields, 3D curl, line integrals, conservative fields (CEO-16).

Rejected:
- Derived counts in page copy (CEO-9: nothing live to derive).
- A depth gate on flagship courses (CEO-38: the report already shows depth).
- Embedding third-party viewers (Alternatives: a second renderer and look).

#### What already exists

See 0B. In short: the 3D stage, materials and primitives; the equation panel, solver and
units; the word-problem bar and parser shape; the function plot; the curriculum build and
spec-type registry; the topics page and deep-link parser; the coverage page. All reused;
`PlotStage` is extracted from the function plot rather than written fresh.

#### Dream state delta

After this plan: 73 models across five subjects, a walkable course browser, a registry a
classifier can target, and usage per course being recorded once the founder creates the
table. Still missing from the 12-month ideal: the second and third representation per
concept, the student's own problem as the entry point, pages that search can find, and
evidence that students return.

#### Failure modes registry

| Codepath | Failure mode | Rescued | Test | User sees | Logged |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| resolver | unknown type | Y | Y | notice | n/a |
| resolver | unknown preset | Y | Y | default preset | n/a |
| Try catalog import | load fails | Y | Y (resolver case) | model from path | n/a |
| model chunk import | load fails | Y | manual | notice with retry | console |
| preset validation | refused | Y | Y (every preset validates) | refusal notice | n/a |
| widget render | throws | Y | manual, sweep | crash notice | console |
| `safeExpression` | refused input | Y | Y | inline message | n/a |
| topics catalog import | load fails | Y | manual | error line with retry | console |
| usage insert | rejected | Y | Y | nothing, by design | dev console, coverage page |
| curriculum build | bad mapping or gate | n/a | Y (fixtures) | n/a | command output |

Rows with RESCUED=N, TEST=N and a silent user outcome: 0. Critical gaps: 0.

Accepted: E2 concept context and next link; E3 topics search; E4 interactive-only toggle
and counts; E7 coverage base layer; E1 usage logging (reopened); E9 CI (reopened); E6 in
its one-line form. Deferred: E5 shareable state; E6 multi-step challenges; path routes.
Skipped: E8 derived counts.

Diagrams produced: system architecture and data flow with shadow paths (Section 1 and 4),
state machine (Section 1), error flow (Section 2 table), user flow (Section 11).
Deployment sequence and rollback are prose in Section 9: one step each. Stale diagram
audit: the plan touches no file that contains an ASCII diagram.

### Design phase (via /autoplan)

A model is done when it has:\n\n1. **Spec**: a TypeScript interface and named presets (at least a default). A spec is a flat\n   record of numbers, strings, enums and arrays of those, so a JSON schema can be generated\n   from it later without hand-authoring. LaTeX strings are constants in code and are never built\n   from anything a student typed.\n4. **Word-problem parser** where the catalog's \"WP\" column says yes: a regex parser that\n   fills the knowns and selects the asked-for unknown, offline. Where a model has a parser\n   but no equation set, the parser only fills the widget's inputs.\n5. **Widget** on one of three stages, all in the same palette:\n   - `3D`: `Scene3D` in the clean-3D look (matte pearl/chrome, ONE glass-accent focal\n     object, azure / warm-azure vectors from `PALETTE`), with a 2D SVG fallback from the\n     same maths when WebGL is missing. `frameloop=\"demand\"` unless animating. No `Label3D`\n     inside a demand-frameloop scene; labels there are HTML laid over the canvas or a\n     legend beside it (the existing `ps__legend` pattern).\n   - `plot`: the shared `PlotStage` (axes, curves, probe point, shaded regions) for\n     graph-shaped models.\n   - `board`: a flat SVG board for discrete or tabular models (grids, tables, step lists).\n   Every stage: motion gated on `prefers-reduced-motion`, nothing that jitters on hover,\n   every control reachable by keyboard, usable at 375px.\n6. **One primary interaction per model.** Variations are presets of that interaction. A\n   second, different interaction is a second model.\n7. **Registration**: a `meta.ts` and a `manifest.ts` in the model's folder; the registry is\n   generated from the folder listing.
