Scope: the Try page (`#/try`), the topics page (`#/topics`), and the stage, controls and
states of 65 new model widgets. Mode for every surface here is OPERATE (app UI): the
student is doing a task, so scanability and native behaviour beat expression.

#### Step 0

- Initial design completeness: 4/10. The plan said what data each screen carries and
  specified a deep-link table and some error copy, but not the Try page's layout, the
  controls a student touches, the board stage, the fallbacks, or most states. A 10 for
  this plan names the frame, the control vocabulary, every state, the gesture and its
  keyboard equivalent for each model, and what each 3D model shows without WebGL.
- Design system: there is no token document. `docs/DESIGN.md` is the product design doc.
  The system lives in code: custom properties in `src/index.css` (cool obsidian ground,
  pearl ink, glass, chrome gradients, one azure accent, 18px radius, the `--ease` curve),
  three typefaces (Clash Display, General Sans, JetBrains Mono), the `.viz` variable set
  in `src/engine/visualize.css`, and `PALETTE` in `materials.ts`. Reviews calibrate
  against those.
- Existing patterns to reuse: the `.pmodel` layout (word-problem bar, stage, equation
  column), `EquationPanel`, `WordProblemBar`, the `ps__legend`, `ps__readout` and
  `ps__transport` blocks, `Scene3D` with its axis-snap buttons, and on topics the tabs,
  course accordions, chips, skeleton rows and error line.
- Drift found in the surface being rebuilt: `.viz` sets its body font to a system stack
  instead of the body font variable, and the topic pill still carries the warm tint of
  the older look. Both are fixed by the frame rebuild.
- Mockups: not generated. The designer binary is installed but reports "No OpenAI API
  key found", which the methodology treats as unavailable. Text review only.
- Focus areas: all seven passes (auto-decided, P1).

#### Design voices

CLAUDE SUBAGENT (design, independent review; input hash 9accd116, full read confirmed):
2 critical, 12 high, 6 medium. Critical: the Try page layout is undefined and conflicts
with the current fixed-height page (C1); there is no shared control, readout or frame kit,
so 65 parallel builds would produce 65 slider dialects (C2). High: an inert topics first
view; boundary states not classed as refuse or teach; typed-expression input with no
states; a stepper with no caption and no shared transport; undefined 2D fallbacks for
3D-only content; continuation dead ends; unreachable presets; a concept link that may not
show its concept; a two-accent palette for subjects that need categories; "board" meaning
three things; parameters listed instead of gestures; verification that checks numbers but
not look.

CODEX SAYS (design, UX challenge): unavailable, CLI not installed.

```
DESIGN OUTSIDE VOICES — LITMUS SCORECARD:
  Check                                    Claude    Codex  Consensus
  1. Brand unmistakable in first screen?   YES       N/A    N/A
  2. One strong visual anchor?             YES       N/A    N/A
  3. Scannable by headlines only?          YES       N/A    N/A
  4. Each section has one job?             YES       N/A    N/A
  5. Cards actually necessary?             YES       N/A    N/A
  6. Motion improves hierarchy?            YES       N/A    N/A
  7. Premium without decorative shadows?   NOT SPEC'D N/A   N/A
  Hard rejections triggered:               0         N/A    N/A
[subagent-only] Outside unavailable: no cell is CONFIRMED.
```

Evidence for the answers, after the fixes below: the Saras mark and the design language
carry the brand while the concept title is the heading (1); the stage is the anchor (2);
topics reads by course and unit headings (3); header, stage, controls, equations each do
one thing (4); the course accordion is the interaction, and nothing else is a card (5);
motion exists only inside models, where it is the content (6); the frame uses no
decorative shadow, and glass is reserved for one object per scene, but the plan does not
state a shadow rule for the frame itself (7).

#### Pass 1: Information architecture. 4/10, now 9/10

- Fixed (structural): one `ModelFrame` with a stated reading order, a fixed header height
  at 1280px, and a pinned stage at 375px. The concept title is the page's one heading.
- Fixed (structural): topics makes the concept with a model the prominent chip, puts an
  interactive count on each tab, and replaces "0 of M" with a muted "none yet".
- Fixed (structural): search covers all five subjects, with per-tab match counts.
- Remaining gap to 10: the frame has no rendered mockup to check the two-line note and
  the previous/next row against real concept titles.

#### Pass 2: Interaction states. 5/10, now 9/10

| Feature | Loading | Empty | Error | Success | Partial |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Try stage | outline skeleton, fixed size | n/a | didn't load (reload), couldn't verify, hit an error (reset), no such model | the model | "Showing the 2D view" |
| Try header | model label first | no next at the end of the course ("Back to <course>") | concept ignored, label shown | title, course, unit, position | n/a |
| Typed expression | last valid picture stays | preset function shown | offending token named under the field | new picture | n/a |
| Word problem | n/a | example offered | "Couldn't find the numbers" | "Set ..." | "Set ... Couldn't find the angle." |
| Topics panel | skeleton courses | four empty-state lines, each with a way forward | error line with retry | accordions with counts | filter active |
| Unmapped chip | n/a | n/a | n/a | "Noted" | n/a |

- Fixed (structural): the refuse-or-teach rule and the list of boundaries that are drawn.
- Fixed (structural): `Transport` shared by time-based models; `Stepper` caption, counter
  and replay; reduced motion becomes manual scrub.
- Fixed (structural): named 2D fallback per 3D model; context loss switches to it.
- Remaining gap: boundary behaviour is listed for 9 models; the other models' edges are
  left to the clamp rule.

#### Pass 3: User journey. 4/10, now 8/10

| Step | Student does | Student feels | Plan specifies |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | opens topics | "is my course here?" | five tabs with interactive counts; prominent chips |
| 2 | searches a concept | impatient | cross-subject search; "1 in Physics" pointer |
| 3 | clicks a quiet chip | mild disappointment | "Noted" and a logged request, so the click is not wasted |
| 4 | clicks "Try" | anticipation | the model renders at once from the path |
| 5 | reads the header | oriented | course, unit, concept title, one prompt |
| 6 | follows the prompt | curiosity, then "oh" | "change X, watch Y: you should see Z"; the picture stays in view at 375px |
| 7 | pushes a control to its edge | testing it | the boundary is drawn and named |
| 8 | wants the next idea | momentum | previous, next, "2 of 5", next unit at the end |
| 9 | goes back to the list | not lost | topics reopens on the same course, search and toggle |

- Five seconds: the stage and one sentence. Five minutes: one concept worked, the next
  one click away. Longer: the course a student is taking, walkable unit by unit.
- Remaining gap: nothing confirms the student saw what the prompt promised; the
  `interacted` event tells the founder a control moved, not that it clicked.

#### Pass 4: AI slop risk. 6/10, now 8/10

- No hard rejection applies: no card grid as first impression, no hero, no carousel; the
  one card-like element (the course accordion) is the interaction.
- Fixed (design system): the system-font fallback and warm tint in `.viz`.
- Fixed (structural): a kit for controls and readouts replaces "each widget styles its own
  sliders", which is how 65 widgets end up looking assembled.
- Fixed (structural): model CSS may use only the existing variables; a check fails on a
  literal colour or a raw range input in a model folder.
- TASTE: element, charge and series colours follow the field's convention in hue and the
  house palette in lightness and saturation. The alternative is a fully house palette
  that ignores convention; it would look more unified and teach worse.
- Remaining gap: no shadow rule is written for the frame, and no mockup exists.

#### Pass 5: Design system alignment. 5/10, now 8/10

- The plan now names its token sources and forbids literals in kit and model CSS.
- New components (`ModelFrame`, the controls kit, `Transport`, `ExpressionInput`,
  `BoardStage`, `PlotStage`) are extractions or siblings of existing blocks, not a new
  vocabulary.
- Gap kept open: there is still no written token document. Deferred as a TODO
  (`/design-consultation`), since writing one is its own piece of work.

#### Pass 6: Responsive and accessibility. 5/10, now 9/10

- 375px: the stage is pinned at 45% of the screen while controls scroll beneath; tables
  wider than a board scroll inside it with the first column pinned; controls are at
  least 44px tall; a 3D stage does not trap one-finger scrolling.
- Keyboard: every control, including anything dragged on the stage; the topics tabs keep
  their roving tabindex; quiet chips are buttons with a pressed state; search and the
  toggle are labelled.
- Screen readers: one heading per page; readouts in a polite live region; the stage has
  a one-sentence description of its current state.
- Reduced motion: time becomes a scrub; no autoplay; no skeleton shimmer.
- Remaining gap: no stated contrast check for the new series ramp beyond "validated".

#### Pass 7: Decisions

| Decision | Resolved as |
|---|---|
| Try page layout and heading | one frame; concept title is the heading (auto, structural) |
| Control vocabulary | controls kit; no raw inputs in model folders (auto, structural) |
| Preset reachability | no orphan presets; in-model cases use the choice control; no preset picker (TASTE; the reviewer proposed a "Variations" row) |
| "Interactive only" default | off (TASTE; the strategy reviewer proposed on) |
| Clicking a concept with no model | marks it "Noted" and logs a request (TASTE) |
| Colour for elements, charges, series | convention sets hue, house palette sets lightness (TASTE) |
| Full page reload on every navigation | removed; `Root` re-renders on hash change (auto; referred to Eng for risk) |
| With `?c=`, wait for the catalog or render at once | render at once, swap only on mismatch (auto) |
| Boundary behaviour | clamp, draw the meaningful ones, refuse only nonsense (auto) |
| 2D fallback per 3D model | named in the plan (auto) |
| Touch orbit | off until "Rotate" is tapped (auto) |
| Search scope | all five subjects (auto) |
| Look verification | screenshots to the founder with each PR; a design checklist for the second agent (auto) |

Resolved: 13. Deferred: 2 (a written token document; mockups).

#### NOT in scope (design)

- A written design-token document: its own piece of work; TODO.
- Mockups: the generator has no API key on this machine.
- A model picker on the Try page: topics is the menu.
- A light theme, and tablet-specific layouts beyond the side panel narrowing.

#### What already exists (design)

Listed under Step 0. Everything new is extracted from or sits beside an existing block.

|---|---|---|---|
| DES-1 | `ModelFrame` and reading order (C1) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P5 structural |
| DES-2 | Controls kit; no raw inputs or literal colours in model folders (C2) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P5 structural |
| DES-3 | Topics hierarchy: prominent and quiet chips, tab counts, "none yet" (H1) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P5 structural |
| DES-4 | "Noted" on a quiet chip, logged as a request (H1) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P1; TASTE |
| DES-5 | "Interactive only" off by default (H1; CEO-38) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P5; TASTE |
| DES-6 | Cross-subject search and the four empty states (M1, M2) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P1 |
| DES-7 | Refuse-or-teach rule; nine drawn boundaries; three parser outcomes (H2) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P1 |
| DES-8 | `ExpressionInput` (H3) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P5 |
| DES-9 | `Transport`; `Stepper` caption and counter; reduced motion as scrub (H4) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P1 |
| DES-10 | Named 2D fallbacks; notice; context-loss switch (H5) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P1 |
| DES-11 | Previous, next, position, unit end; topics state in the address (H6) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P1 |
| DES-12 | No full reload on navigation (H6) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P5; referred to Eng |
| DES-13 | No orphan presets; choice control for in-model cases; no preset picker (H7; reverses nothing, narrows CEO-31's "no picker") | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P5; TASTE |
| DES-14 | The linked concept is visible at load; optional per-concept note (H8) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P1 |
| DES-15 | Palette additions; convention sets hue (H9) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P5; TASTE |
| DES-16 | `BoardStage` with canvas and table layouts (H10) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P5 |
| DES-17 | Gestures, keyboard parity, touch "Rotate" (H11) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P1 |
| DES-18 | Screenshots and a design checklist per PR (H12) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P1 |
| DES-19 | Note format, `interacted` event, disclosure line, copy rules, render at once with `?c=` (M3 to M6) | approved | /autoplan auto-decision, P1 |
| DES-20 | Written token document | deferred | /autoplan auto-decision, P3; pending TODO write |

<!-- autoplan-accepted:design -->
- Record note: every requirement below is written into the plan sections above ("Try page frame", "Topics page as a course browser", "Stage details", the recipe, "Usage logging"). This list is the record of what the design review accepted.
- Try page frame (Wave 0): one `ModelFrame`, extracted from the existing physics-model layout. Header in reading order: course and unit label, concept title as the page's only heading (model label when no concept), a "try this" note of at most two lines, previous and next concept links with position. Fixed header height at 1280px so the page never scrolls; at 375px the stage is pinned at 45% of the screen while controls scroll beneath. The Saras mark links home. Tab title "<concept title> · Saras". Body text uses the body font variable; the warm tint and system-font fallback in `.viz` are removed.
- Navigation: previous and next follow catalog order within the unit and skip unmapped concepts; at a unit's last mapped concept next becomes the next unit's first mapped concept, or "Back to <course>". "All topics" returns to the same subject, search, toggle and open course. `Root` re-renders on hash change and the Try page re-resolves in place, so no full page reload between concepts.
- Controls kit (Wave 0): slider row, choice, number field with unit, readout row, legend, reset. It is the only source of controls; in-model cases use the choice control. No model picker and no preset picker. `scripts/check-models.test.ts` fails on a raw range or number input or a literal colour in a model folder. One number format through `fmt`: three significant figures, scientific notation below 0.001 and from 100,000, unit after a thin space, en dash for a missing value.
- Other Wave 0 kits: `Transport` (play, pause, scrub, speed) for every time-based model; `ExpressionInput` (debounced, keeps the last valid picture, names the offending token; presets with sliders first, typing second). Wave 1: `BoardStage` with `canvas` and `table` layouts, 12px minimum text, wide tables scrolling inside the board with the first column pinned; `Stepper` gains a caption line, a step counter and replay.
- Replaced from the CEO record: the stage no longer waits for the catalog when `c` is present (it renders from the path at once); search is across all subjects, not the active one; the two empty-state lines become four; a failed chunk offers a page reload, not an in-place retry; previous and position join the next link; pages no longer reload on a hash change.
- Stage states: loading (outline skeleton, fixed size, no shimmer under reduced motion), ready, chunk failed ("This model didn't load." with a page reload), refused ("Couldn't verify a correct visual for this" with the reason), crashed ("This model hit an error." with reset and a link to topics), no such model ("No model called X", X shortened to 40 characters). With `?c=` the model named by the path renders at once and swaps only if the concept maps elsewhere.
- Boundaries: controls clamp; a meaningful boundary is drawn and labelled (the nine listed in "Stage details"); only nonsense is refused, with the reason beside the control. A word-problem parse has three outcomes, each with its own line: filled, partial with the missing quantity named, not understood.
- Presets and notes: every preset is the default or mapped to a concept (registry test). A note has the form "Change X and watch Y: you should see Z". A concept maps to a preset that shows that concept at load, with its own note when the preset's does not name it; the second agent checks this.
- 3D: each 3D model has the one glass object and the 2D fallback named in "Stage details"; a fallback announces itself ("Showing the 2D view"); WebGL loss switches to it; on touch, orbiting is off until "Rotate" is tapped.
- Accessibility: every control keyboard-reachable, including stage draggables (arrow-key nudging and a mirrored number field); controls at least 44px tall at 375px; readouts in a polite live region; a one-sentence state description on the stage; under reduced motion time becomes a manual scrub and `ParticleBox` shows a still snapshot with a step button; nothing told apart by colour alone.
- Colour (before Wave 1): a six-step series ramp, a positive/negative pair, a reactant/product pair and element colours, defined once in `materials.ts` and the `.viz` variables; convention sets hue, the house palette sets lightness and saturation. Model CSS uses only existing variables.
- Topics page (Wave 0): interactive count on each tab; a concept with a model is the prominent chip, one without is a quiet chip that becomes "Noted" on click and logs `want`; search across all five subjects with per-tab match counts; "interactive only" switch, off by default; the four empty states in the plan; "N of M interactive" or a muted "none yet" per course; subject, query, toggle and open course kept in the address.
- Usage events: `open`, `interacted` (first control change), `engaged`, and `want`. A footer line on the Try and topics pages: "We count which models get opened. No names, no accounts."
- Verification: the second agent checks each model against the design checklist in the recipe; screenshots of each new model at 375px and 1280px, 3D and fallback, go to the founder with the PR.
- Deferred, to be written to TODOS.md on approval: a written design-token document.
<!-- /autoplan-accepted:design -->

<!-- AUTONOMOUS DECISION LOG -->
### DX phase (via /autoplan)

Wave 0 seeds\n  that list with one \"pending Wave N\" entry per course not yet covered (table below); each\n  wave deletes the entries it satisfies; after Wave 6 the list may contain only permanent\n  exclusions, each with a reason. The gate also fails on an entry for a course that is\n  already mapped, and on an entry naming a course that does not exist.\n- **Report** (printed by the build, never a gate): per-course coverage, and every high\n  3D-fit concept that is still unmapped.\n\nExpected coverage after all waves is roughly 250 to 300 of 734 concepts: the families\nnamed in the catalog below. A model is done when it has:\n\n1. **Spec**: a TypeScript interface and named presets (at least a default). A spec is a flat\n   record of numbers, strings, enums and arrays of those, so a JSON schema can be generated\n   from it later without hand-authoring. Every preset carries a one-sentence \"try this\"\n   prompt in the spec's `notes` field, in the form \"Change X and watch Y: you should see\n   Z\". Every preset is either the model's default or mapped to at least one concept, so no\n   preset is reachable only by typing a URL.\n2. **Deterministic core**: the closed-form (or otherwise deterministic) maths in one place,\n   used by BOTH the validation gate and the renderer, so what is drawn is what was checked.\n   `validate(spec)` refuses non-physical or out-of-range input with a plain reason.\n   Controls clamp to the valid range, so a student cannot reach a refused state by\n   dragging. A boundary that means something (total internal reflection, a triangle with\n   no solution, a singular matrix) is drawn and labelled, not refused. Only nonsense is\n   refused, and the reason appears next to the control that caused it.\n3. **Equation set** where the catalog's \"Eq\" column says yes: LaTeX/KaTeX display, residual\n   form, solve-for-any-variable through `useEquationPanel`, canonical-SI units with the\n   unit dropdown from `units.ts`. LaTeX strings are constants in code and are never built\n   from anything a student typed.\n4. **Word-problem parser** where the catalog's \"WP\" column says yes: a regex parser that\n   fills the knowns and selects the asked-for unknown, offline. Where a model has a parser\n   but no equation set, the parser only fills the widget's inputs. A parse has three\n   outcomes, each with its own line under the input: filled (\"Set v = 12 m/s, angle =\n   30 degrees\"), partial (\"Set v = 12 m/s. Couldn't find the angle.\"), and not understood\n   (\"Couldn't find the numbers\", with an example to try).\n5. **Widget** on one of three stages, all in the same palette:\n   - `3D`: `Scene3D` in the clean-3D look (matte pearl/chrome, ONE glass-accent focal\n     object, azure / warm-azure vectors from `PALETTE`), with a 2D SVG fallback from the\n     same maths when WebGL is missing. `frameloop=\"demand\"` unless animating.
