The generator's\ncomplete-folder rule is tested in `scripts/gen.test.ts` (tests under `scripts/` may use\nnode APIs; the runner executes them too).\n\n- `src/coverage/CurriculumCoverage.tsx` uses the real mapping as the base layer. A real\n  mapping wins and is read-only there; the localStorage planning layer applies only to\n  concepts with no real mapping.\n\n"},{"oldText":"### Deep links\n\nThe address is read again whenever the hash changes; nothing here needs a page reload.\n\n| URL | Resolves to |\n|---|---|\n| `#/try` | `projectile`, its default preset (today's default); header shows the model label |\n| `#/try/<type>` | that model's default preset; header shows the model label |\n| `#/try/<type>/<preset>` | that preset; header shows the model label and preset label |\n| any of the above with `?c=<conceptId>`, id known and mapped | the `(type, preset)` that concept maps to (the concept wins over the path); header shows concept title, course and unit, and a link to the next mapped concept in the unit when one exists |\n| `?c=` with an unknown or unmapped id | `c` is ignored; resolved from the path |\n| known type, unknown preset | the type's default preset; header shows the model label |\n| unknown type | a \"No model called X\" notice with a link to topics; nothing is rendered in its place |\n\nThe header's concept data comes from `catalog/<subject>.json`, loaded lazily using the\nsubject prefix of the concept id. The model named by the path renders at once; when the\ncatalog arrives the header fills in, and the model is swapped only in the rare case that\nthe concept maps somewhere else. If the catalog fails to load, or the prefix is not a\nsubject, `c` is treated as unknown: the header shows the model label and no previous or\nnext links. Previous, next and position follow catalog order and skip unmapped concepts\n(see \"Try page frame\" for the unit-end behaviour).\n\nTests: a unit test of the pure resolver with one case per table row, and of the\nprevious, next and position lookups (middle of unit, first, last, last in course,\nunknown id).\n\n","newText":"### Deep links\n\nThe address is read again whenever the hash changes; nothing here needs a page reload.\n\n| URL | Resolves to |\n|---|---|\n| `#/try` | `projectile`, its default preset (today's default); header shows the model label |\n| `#/try/<type>` | that model's default preset; header shows the model label |\n| `#/try/<type>/<preset>` | that preset; header shows the model label and preset label |\n| any of the above with `?c=<conceptId>`, id known and mapped | the `(type, preset)` that concept maps to (the concept wins over the path); header shows concept title, course and unit, and a link to the next mapped concept in the unit when one exists |\n| `#/try?c=<conceptId>` with no type in the path | the stage shows its skeleton until the catalog has loaded, then the concept's model (never `projectile` first) |\n| `?c=` with an unknown or unmapped id | `c` is ignored; resolved from the path |\n| a path or value that cannot be decoded (a stray `%`) | treated as an unknown type; the notice, never a thrown error |\n| known type, unknown preset | the type's default preset; header shows the model label |\n| unknown type | a \"No model called X\" notice with a link to topics; nothing is rendered in its place |\n\nThe header's concept data comes from the catalog, loaded lazily. The model named by the\npath renders at once; when the catalog arrives the header fills in, and the model is\nswapped only in the rare case that the concept maps somewhere else. The `open` event is\nsent once, after the address has settled. If the catalog fails to load, `c` is treated\nas unknown: the header shows the model label and no previous or\nnext links. Previous, next and position follow catalog order and skip unmapped concepts\n(see \"Try page frame\" for the unit-end behaviour).\n\nTests: a unit test of the pure resolver with one case per table row, and of the\nprevious, next and position lookups (middle of unit, first, last, last in course,\nunknown id).\n\n"},{"oldText":"### Topics page as a course browser\n\n- Reads the generated catalog (one lazy chunk per subject) instead of Supabase. The topics\n  page no longer depends on the database; the seed script keeps mirroring the curriculum\n  for relational use. The live-table column check added on this branch goes away with the\n  query it guarded.\n- Five subject tabs (adds Computing), each showing how many of its concepts are\n  interactive. Courses, units and concepts appear in catalog order.\n- A concept with a model is the prominent chip: full-strength text, the azure edge it has\n  today, and its \"Try →\" link, `#/try/<type>/<preset>?c=<conceptId>`. A concept without\n  one is a quiet chip. Clicking a quiet chip marks it \"Noted\" for the session and records\n  a `want` event; it is a button with a pressed state, so it works by keyboard.\n- Search box, labelled \"Search concepts\": case-insensitive title substring across all\n  five subjects. While a query is active each tab shows its match count.\n- \"Interactive only\" toggle, a labelled switch, off by default: hides concepts with no\n  model.\n- Search and toggle combine with AND. While either is active, units and courses with no\n  matching concept are hidden and courses with a match are expanded.\n- Empty states, each one line with a way forward:\n  - a query that matches nothing in this subject but something elsewhere: \"No matches in\n    Math. 1 in Physics\", linking to that tab;\n  - a query that matches nothing anywhere: \"No concepts match\";\n  - a query that matches only concepts without models while the toggle is on: \"3 concepts\n    match, none interactive yet. Show all\", which turns the toggle off;\n  - no query, toggle on, nothing interactive: \"No interactive models in this subject yet\".\n- Each course header shows \"N of M interactive\", counted over the whole course whatever\n  the search or toggle says; a course with none shows a muted \"none yet\".\n- The subject, query, toggle and open course live in the address\n  (`#/topics?s=math&q=slope&i=1&u=algebra-1`), so \"all topics\" on the Try page returns\n  the student to where they were.\n- If a subject's catalog fails to load, the panel shows the existing error line with a\n  retry button.\n\nTests: unit tests of the pure filter and count functions (match, no match, empty query,\nmixed case, toggle alone, toggle with search, match only in another subject, match with\nnothing interactive), of reading and writing the address state, and a browser check that\nwalks two next hops through \"Forms of linear equations\" and returns by \"all topics\" to\nthe same open course.\n\n","newText":"### Topics page as a course browser\n\n- Reads the generated catalog (one lazy chunk) instead of Supabase. The topics\n  page no longer depends on the database; the seed script keeps mirroring the curriculum\n  for relational use. The live-table column check added on this branch goes away with the\n  query it guarded.\n- Five subject tabs (adds Computing), each showing how many of its concepts are\n  interactive. Courses, units and concepts appear in catalog order.\n- A concept with a model is the prominent chip: full-strength text, the azure edge it has\n  today, and its \"Try →\" link, `#/try/<type>/<preset>?c=<conceptId>`. A concept without\n  one is a quiet chip. Clicking a quiet chip marks it \"Noted\" for the session and records\n  a `want` event; it is a button with a pressed state, so it works by keyboard.\n- Search box, labelled \"Search concepts\": case-insensitive title substring across all\n  five subjects. While a query is active each tab shows its match count.\n- \"Interactive only\" toggle, a labelled switch, off by default: hides concepts with no\n  model.\n- Search and toggle combine with AND. While either is active, units and courses with no\n  matching concept are hidden and courses with a match are expanded.\n- Empty states, each one line with a way forward:\n  - a query that matches nothing in this subject but something elsewhere: \"No matches in\n    Math. 1 in Physics\", linking to that tab;\n  - a query that matches nothing anywhere: \"No concepts match\";\n  - a query that matches only concepts without models while the toggle is on: \"3 concepts\n    match, none interactive yet. Show all\", which turns the toggle off;\n  - no query, toggle on, nothing interactive: \"No interactive models in this subject yet\".\n- Each course header shows \"N of M interactive\", counted over the whole course whatever\n  the search or toggle says; a course with none shows a muted \"none yet\".\n- The subject, query, toggle and open course live in the address\n  (`#/topics?s=math&q=slope&i=1&u=algebra-1`), so \"all topics\" on the Try page returns\n  the student to where they were.\n- If the catalog fails to load, the page shows the existing error line with a retry\n  button.\n\nTests: unit tests of the pure filter and count functions (match, no match, empty query,\nmixed case, toggle alone, toggle with search, match only in another subject, match with\nnothing interactive), of reading and writing the address state, and a browser check that\nwalks two next hops through \"Forms of linear equations\" and returns by \"all topics\" to\nthe same open course.\n\n"},{"oldText":"### First real mappings (so Wave 0 is verifiable end to end)\n\n`function-grapher` is brought to the full recipe (folder, presets, `PlotStage`, 375px\npass, `built:true`) with presets for the Algebra 1 concepts it shows faithfully: absolute\nvalue functions, graphing linear equations, slope of a line, slope-intercept form,\npoint-slope form, standard form of a line, x- and y-intercepts, graphing parabolas, vertex\nform of a quadratic, exponential functions, exponential growth and decay models. Its test\nasserts every preset passes `validateFunctionGrapher` (which tolerates asymptotes) and that\nthese eleven, which have none, are finite at every sample across their domain. The unit\n\"Forms of linear equations\" then has three mapped concepts in a row, which is what the\nnext-in-unit browser check walks.\n\n","newText":"### First real mappings (so Wave 0 is verifiable end to end)\n\n`function-grapher` is brought to the full recipe (folder, presets, `PlotStage`, 375px\npass, `built:true`) with presets for the Algebra 1 concepts it shows faithfully: absolute\nvalue functions, graphing linear equations, slope of a line, slope-intercept form,\npoint-slope form, standard form of a line, x- and y-intercepts, graphing parabolas, vertex\nform of a quadratic, exponential functions, exponential growth and decay models. Its test\nasserts every preset passes the model's `validate` (which tolerates asymptotes) and that\nthese eleven, which have none, are finite at every sample across their domain. The unit\n\"Forms of linear equations\" then has three mapped concepts in a row, which is what the\nnext-in-unit browser check walks.\n\n"},{"oldText":"### The builder's path and the docs\n\nThe people who use this structure are the agents that each build one model, the agent\nthat checks it, the integrator, and the founder. Their first five minutes, from a fresh\ngit worktree:\n\n| Step | Command | Result |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `bun run setup:worktree` | installs, copies `.env.local` from the main checkout when there is one, picks a free port and saves it for this worktree, runs the generator, prints the address to open |\n| 2 | `bun dev` | the dev server on that port |\n| 3 | open `#/try/<type>` | the model's stub, live inside the real frame (the integrator scaffolded it with `bun run new:model`) |\n| 4 | edit `core.ts` or `Widget.tsx` | the picture changes on save |\n| 5 | `bun run test <type>` and `bun run sweep <type>` | the model's own assertions; its presets at both widths, with and without WebGL |\n\nTarget: steps 1 to 3 in under five minutes, most of it the install. `vite.config.ts` reads\nthe saved port and refuses to start on a busy one, so a builder never ends up looking at\nanother worktree's server.\n\nDocs written in Wave 0, because 65 builders will read them and none of them wrote the\nfoundation:\n\n- `docs/MODELS.md`, the authoring guide: a glossary with one word per thing (model, model\n  type, spec, preset, widget, stage, kit, registry, catalog, concept; the older names\n  `specTypes.ts` and `VisualizePanel` are mapped onto them); the five steps above; a\n  copy-paste skeleton for each file; `projectile` and `function-grapher` walked through as\n  worked examples; a table of every command; the kit-request and declined-concept rules;\n  and one anchored entry per message in \"Messages a builder sees\". A dated \"Changes\"\n  list at the top records every change to the contract or a kit.\n- `docs/MODEL-REVIEW.md`, the second agent's checklist (the recipe's verification and\n  design checks and the reference-data check) with a fixed pass/fail report format, so\n  every review reads the same.\n- `docs/MODEL-BRIEF.md`, the template the integrator fills in for each builder: the\n  model's row from the catalog, its assigned concepts, its stage and kits, the files it\n  may touch (its own folder only), the commands to run, and what the hand-off report must\n  contain (what was built, anything declined, any kit request, and what slowed the\n  builder down).\n- `README.md`, `CLAUDE.md` and `src/data/curriculum/README.md` are corrected: the project\n  structure, the full command list (`test`, `typecheck`, `gen:curriculum`, `new:model`,\n  `sweep`, `setup:worktree`), the model count, and how the curriculum is regenerated.\n  Counts in docs are copied from the build's report, not typed from memory.\n\nThe contract and the kits change only through the integrator. A change to `types.ts`\nshows up as a type error in every model it affects. A breaking kit change is made\ntogether with the edits to every model that uses it, in one PR, and noted in the\n\"Changes\" list.\n\nAfter Wave 1 the integrator records, beside the effort actuals, the time from a fresh\nworktree to the stub on screen and the \"what slowed me down\" lines from the builders'\nreports, and fixes the guide or the scaffold before Wave 2.\n\nTests: `scripts/setup-worktree.test.ts` (the port is free and saved; an existing\n`.env.local` is not overwritten).\n\n","newText":"### The builder's path and the docs\n\nThe people who use this structure are the agents that each build one model, the agent\nthat checks it, the integrator, and the founder. Their first five minutes, from a fresh\ngit worktree:\n\n| Step | Command | Result |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `bun run setup:worktree` | installs, copies only the browser-safe (`VITE_`) lines of the main checkout's `.env.local` when there is one (never the server key), picks a port from this worktree's path and checks it is free, saves it, runs the generator, prints the address to open |\n| 2 | `bun dev` | the dev server on that port |\n| 3 | open `#/try/<type>` | the model's stub, live inside the real frame (the integrator scaffolded it with `bun run new:model`) |\n| 4 | edit `core.ts` or `Widget.tsx` | the picture changes on save |\n| 5 | `bun run test <type>` and `bun run sweep <type>` | the model's own assertions; its presets at both widths, with and without WebGL |\n| 6 | `bun run check:branch <type>` | confirms the branch changed nothing outside the model's folder |\n\nTarget: steps 1 to 3 in under five minutes, most of it the install. `vite.config.ts` reads\nthe saved port and refuses to start on a busy one, and the dev server answers a small\nidentity request with its worktree's path, which the sweep checks, so neither a builder\nnor the sweep ends up looking at another worktree's server.\n\nDocs written in Wave 0, because 65 builders will read them and none of them wrote the\nfoundation:\n\n- `docs/MODELS.md`, the authoring guide: a glossary with one word per thing (model, model\n  type, spec, preset, widget, stage, kit, registry, catalog, concept; the older names\n  `specTypes.ts` and `VisualizePanel` are mapped onto them); the steps above; a\n  copy-paste skeleton for each file; `projectile` and `function-grapher` walked through as\n  worked examples; a table of every command; the kit-request and declined-concept rules;\n  and one anchored entry per message in \"Messages a builder sees\". A dated \"Changes\"\n  list at the top records every change to the contract or a kit.\n- `docs/MODEL-REVIEW.md`, the second agent's checklist (the recipe's verification and\n  design checks and the reference-data check) with a fixed pass/fail report format, so\n  every review reads the same.\n- `docs/MODEL-BRIEF.md`, the template the integrator fills in for each builder: the\n  model's row from the catalog, its assigned concepts, its stage and kits, the files it\n  may touch (its own folder only), the commands to run, and what the hand-off report must\n  contain (what was built, anything declined, any kit request, and what slowed the\n  builder down).\n- `README.md`, `CLAUDE.md` and `src/data/curriculum/README.md` are corrected: the project\n  structure, the full command list (`test`, `typecheck`, `gen:curriculum`, `new:model`,\n  `sweep`, `setup:worktree`), the model count, and how the curriculum is regenerated.\n  Counts in docs are copied from the build's report, not typed from memory.\n\nThe contract and the kits change only through the integrator. A change to `types.ts`\nshows up as a type error in every model it affects. A breaking kit change is made\ntogether with the edits to every model that uses it, in one PR, and noted in the\n\"Changes\" list.\n\nAfter Wave 1 the integrator records, beside the effort actuals, the time from a fresh\nworktree to the stub on screen and the \"what slowed me down\" lines from the builders'\nreports, and fixes the guide or the scaffold before Wave 2.\n\nTests: `scripts/setup-worktree.test.ts` (the port is free and saved; an existing\n`.env.local` is not overwritten).\n\n"},{"oldText":"### Messages a builder sees\n\nEvery check that can stop a builder says what is wrong, with the actual value; where, by\nfile and id; and what to do. Each has an entry in `docs/MODELS.md`.\n\n| Check | Example |\n|---|---|\n| generator, incomplete folder | `skipped unit-circle: Widget.tsx is missing (found widget.tsx; names are case-sensitive). A model needs meta.ts, core.ts, presets.ts and Widget.tsx.` |\n| generator, no default export | `unit-circle/presets.ts has no default export. Expected: export default [...] satisfies Preset<UnitCircleSpec>[]` |\n| mapping, unknown concept | `unit-circle/concepts.ts maps \"math.algebra-2.unit-circl\", which is not a concept. Closest id: \"math.algebra-2.unit-circle-a2\".` |\n| mapping, two models | `\"physics.ap-physics-1.buoyancy\" is mapped by both buoyancy and fluid-flow. A concept has one model: remove it from one concepts.ts.` |\n| mapping, missing preset | `unit-circle/concepts.ts maps \"math.algebra-2.unit-circle-a2\" to preset \"radians\", but presets.ts has: default, special-angles.` |\n| mapping, raw file | `raw/physics.json sets specType on \"physics.ap-physics-1.buoyancy\". Mappings live in a model's concepts.ts: delete the field.` |\n| gate, uncovered course | `Course \"geometry\" has no mapped concept and no entry in exclusions.ts. Map a concept, or add an entry with a reason.` |\n| gate, pending entry now covered | `exclusions.ts still lists \"algebra-2\" as pending, but unit-circle maps into it. Delete that entry.` (a warning in a model branch, an error in check mode) |\n| gate, unknown course | `exclusions.ts names course \"algebra-3\", which does not exist. Did you mean \"algebra-2\"?` |\n| check mode, subject JSON | `physics.json differs from what raw/physics.json produces. Run bun run gen:curriculum and commit physics.json.` |\n| check mode, stub | `unit-circle is still a scaffold stub. Remove stub: true from meta.ts when the model meets the recipe.` |\n| check mode, kit request | `unit-circle/KIT-REQUEST.md is unanswered. The integrator extends the kit or accepts the local drawing, then deletes the file.` |\n| registry, preset refused | `free-body-diagram preset \"steep\" fails its own validate: \"Angle must be between 0 and 90\" (field: angle).` |\n| registry, orphan preset | `unit-circle preset \"radians\" is not the default and no concept maps to it. Map a concept to it or delete it.` |\n| registry, Eq / WP | `planned.ts says rc-circuit has a word-problem parser, but rc-circuit/parse.ts is missing.` |\n| model rules | `unit-circle/Widget.tsx:41 uses a raw <input>. Use SliderRow or NumberField from the controls kit, or leave a KIT-REQUEST.md.` |\n| model rules, colour | `unit-circle/widget.css:12 uses the literal colour #7fb2ff. Use a colour variable (see materials.ts).` |\n| an assertion | `FAIL projectile range at 45 degrees: expected 40.775 (within 0.004), got 40.12` |\n| wrong test command | `These tests are standalone scripts. Use: bun run test` |\n| port in use | `Port 5183 is in use. Run bun run setup:worktree to pick another.` |\n| no Supabase config | `No .env.local: sign-in and usage logging are off. Models and topics work without it.` |\n\nTests: each message is asserted by the test of the check that produces it.\n\n","newText":"### Messages a builder sees\n\nEvery check that can stop a builder says what is wrong, with the actual value; where, by\nfile and id; and what to do. Map a concept to it or delete it.` |\n| registry, Eq / WP | `planned.ts says rc-circuit has a word-problem parser, but rc-circuit/parse.ts is missing.` |\n| gate, nothing mapped | `unit-circle maps no concept, so nothing links to it. Map a concept or remove the model.` (a warning in a model branch, an error in check mode) |\n| registry, range sampling | `buoyancy: with preset \"default\" and fluidDensity at its minimum (0), derive().floatingFraction is not finite. Raise the range's minimum or return \"undefined here\".` |\n| model rules | `unit-circle/Widget.tsx:41 uses a raw <input>. Use SliderRow or NumberField from the controls kit, or leave a KIT-REQUEST.md.` |\n| model rules, imports | `unit-circle/core.ts imports react. A model's pure files import no React, three or CSS: move the drawing into Widget.tsx.` |\n| branch scope | `This branch changed src/engine/kits/PlotStage.tsx, outside src/engine/models/unit-circle/. A model branch changes only its own folder: leave a KIT-REQUEST.md instead.` |\n| a test with no checks | `unit-circle.test.ts made no assertions. Add checks, or delete the file.` |\n| bundle | `unit-circle (a board model) loads three through kits/ParticleBox/three.tsx. Import the flat renderer instead.` |\n| model rules, colour | `unit-circle/widget.css:12 uses the literal colour #7fb2ff. Models and topics work without it.` |\n\nTests: each message is asserted by the test of the check that produces it.\n\n"},{"oldText":"### Wave 1: finish the designed vocabulary (7 models, completes the original registry)\n\n| Model | Subject | Stage | Student manipulates | Gate | Eq / WP | Concept families |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `unit-circle` | math | board | an angle on the circle; sine, cosine and tangent traces unroll beside it | exact trig values at special angles; sin^2 + cos^2 = 1 | no / no | unit circle, radians, special angles, reference angles, Pythagorean identity, graphs of sine and cosine |\n| `linear-transform` | math | plot | the four entries of a 2x2 matrix; grid, basis vectors and unit square deform | determinant = signed area; eigenpairs from the characteristic polynomial; inverse exists iff det != 0 | no / no | matrices as transformations, determinant as area, eigenvectors, composition, inverses, rotation/reflection/projection matrices |\n| `surface-3d` | math | 3D | z = f(x, y) with parameters; a probe point with tangent plane, gradient arrow and contour projection | mathjs evaluation on a sample grid; numeric partials vs symbolic; finite everywhere drawn | no / no | 3D graphs, level curves, partial derivatives, gradient, tangent planes, multivariable critical points and the second-derivative test |\n| `vector-field` | math | plot | the two components of F(x, y); a probe showing divergence and scalar curl; a dropped particle tracing a flow line | numeric divergence and curl vs closed form on test fields; RK4 flow lines vs known solutions | no / no | vector fields, 2D curl and 2D divergence, divergence of a vector field |\n| `molecule-3d` | chemistry | 3D | a molecule from a curated library; rotate; toggle lone pairs, bond dipoles, hybridization labels | VSEPR class from electron domains; ideal angles exact (109.47, 120, 180); net dipole by vector sum; measured angles stored and labelled as measured | no / no | VSEPR, Lewis structures, bond and molecular polarity, hybridization, intermolecular forces |\n| `reaction-energy` | chemistry, biology | plot | activation energy, enthalpy change, temperature, catalyst on/off | Arrhenius rate ratio; delta-H = products minus reactants; a catalyst lowers Ea and leaves delta-H alone | yes / yes | activation energy and collision theory, catalysis, enthalpy changes, enzymes and activation energy |\n| `step-walkthrough` | computing | board with `Stepper` | an input array or disk count; play and scrub through the steps | steps come from RUNNING the real algorithm, never authored; invariants asserted per step (sorted prefix, search window contains the target, legal Hanoi moves) | no / no | selection, insertion, merge and quick sort; linear and binary search; Towers of Hanoi |\n\nAlso in Wave 1: `function-grapher` presets for the Algebra 2, precalculus and trigonometry\ngraph concepts it shows faithfully (the integrator's concept assignment lists them), and\nthe extensions below.\n\n","newText":"### Wave 1: finish the designed vocabulary (7 models, completes the original registry)\n\n| Model | Subject | Stage | Student manipulates | Gate | Eq / WP | Concept families |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `unit-circle` | math | board | an angle on the circle; sine, cosine and tangent traces unroll beside it | exact trig values at special angles; sin^2 + cos^2 = 1 | no / no | unit circle, radians, special angles, reference angles, Pythagorean identity, graphs of sine and cosine |\n| `linear-transform` | math | plot | the four entries of a 2x2 matrix; grid, basis vectors and unit square deform | determinant = signed area; eigenpairs from the characteristic polynomial; inverse exists iff det != 0 | no / no | matrices as transformations, determinant as area, eigenvectors, composition, inverses, rotation/reflection/projection matrices |\n| `surface-3d` | math | 3D | z = f(x, y) with parameters; a probe point with tangent plane, gradient arrow and contour projection | mathjs evaluation on a sample grid; numeric partials vs symbolic; finite on every cell drawn, with undefined cells left as holes and an en dash at an undefined probe | no / no | 3D graphs, level curves, partial derivatives, gradient, tangent planes, multivariable critical points and the second-derivative test |\n| `vector-field` | math | plot | the two components of F(x, y); delta-H = products minus reactants; a catalyst lowers Ea and leaves delta-H alone | yes / yes | activation energy and collision theory, catalysis, enthalpy changes, enzymes and activation energy |\n| `step-walkthrough` | computing | board with `Stepper` | an input array or disk count; play and scrub through the steps | steps come from RUNNING the real algorithm, never authored; invariants asserted per step (sorted prefix, search window contains the target, legal Hanoi moves) | no / no | selection, insertion, merge and quick sort; linear and binary search; Towers of Hanoi |\n\nAlso in Wave 1: `function-grapher` presets for the Algebra 2, precalculus and trigonometry\ngraph concepts it shows faithfully (the integrator's concept assignment lists them), and\nthe extensions below.\n\n"},{"oldText":"### Existing models: extensions and remapping (Wave 1)\n\nA concept is remapped onto an existing model only when the model, with its extension where\none is listed, shows that concept. Each extension carries assertions in that model's test\nfile. New spec fields are optional with defaults, so the existing schema branches and\nprompt text in `src/engine/spec.ts`, and `api/spec.ts`, stay untouched.\n\n| Existing model | Extension | Concepts it then serves |\n|---|---|---|\n| `circular-motion` | optional mass, and the equation F = m v^2 / r | centripetal force and acceleration; uniform circular motion |\n| `wave-oscillator` | the equation F = k x beside the existing period and energy equations | Hooke's law and springs; simple harmonic motion |\n| `circuit-diagram` | optional named network preset (single loop, series pair, parallel pair) that seeds the existing builder; test asserts Kirchhoff's current and voltage laws hold in the solver's result | Ohm's law and resistance; series and parallel circuits; Kirchhoff's circuit laws |\n| `free-body-diagram` | optional static coefficient (defaults to the existing `friction` field, which stays the kinetic coefficient, so current specs behave exactly as before); optional applied force F along the surface; a flat-surface preset. The block holds while abs(m g sin(theta) - F) is at most mu_s m g cos(theta); otherwise it slides with kinetic friction opposing the motion | forces on an inclined plane; friction (static and kinetic); free-body diagrams |\n| `orbit-sim` | optional orbiting mass and the readouts F = M m / r^2 and U = -M m / r in its natural units | Newton's law of universal gravitation; gravitational potential energy and escape velocity |\n\n`projectile` and `ray-diagram` keep their current mappings. These five extensions edit the\nshared legacy files, so one agent does them in sequence; they are not part of the parallel\nfan-out.\n\n","newText":"### Existing models: extensions and remapping (Wave 1)\n\nA concept is remapped onto an existing model only when the model, with its extension where\none is listed, shows that concept. Each extension carries assertions in that model's test\nfile. New spec fields are optional with defaults, so the existing schema branches and\nprompt text in `src/engine/spec.ts`, and `api/spec.ts`, stay untouched.\n\n| Existing model | Extension | Concepts it then serves |\n|---|---|---|\n| `circular-motion` | optional mass, and the equation F = m v^2 / r | centripetal force and acceleration; uniform circular motion |\n| `wave-oscillator` | the equation F = k x beside the existing period and energy equations | Hooke's law and springs; simple harmonic motion |\n| `circuit-diagram` | optional named network preset (single loop, series pair, parallel pair) that seeds the existing builder; test asserts Kirchhoff's current and voltage laws hold in the solver's result | Ohm's law and resistance; series and parallel circuits; Kirchhoff's circuit laws |\n| `free-body-diagram` | optional static coefficient (defaults to the existing `friction` field, which stays the kinetic coefficient, so current specs behave exactly as before; a static coefficient below the kinetic one is refused); optional applied force F along the surface; a flat-surface preset. The block holds while abs(m g sin(theta) - F) is at most mu_s m g cos(theta); otherwise it slides with kinetic friction opposing the motion | forces on an inclined plane; friction (static and kinetic); free-body diagrams |\n| `orbit-sim` | optional orbiting mass and the readouts F = M m / r^2 and U = -M m / r in its natural units | Newton's law of universal gravitation; gravitational potential energy and escape velocity |\n\n`projectile` and `ray-diagram` keep their current mappings. These five extensions edit the\nshared legacy files, so one agent does them in sequence; they are not part of the parallel\nfan-out.\n\n"},{"oldText":"### Wave 4: chemistry (10 models, on top of `molecule-3d` and `reaction-energy`)\n\n| Model | Stage | Student manipulates | Gate | Eq / WP | Concept families |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `atom-model` | 3D | element; isotope; ion charge | shell capacities 2 n^2; configuration by filling order with the documented exceptions held in data; average atomic mass = weighted sum | no / no | atomic structure, electron configuration, atomic orbitals, ion formation, isotopes and average atomic mass |\n| `titration` | plot | acid and base strength, concentrations, volume added | pH from the exact charge-balance equation; equivalence at equal moles; Henderson-Hasselbalch inside the buffer region | yes / yes | acids, bases and the pH scale; buffers and titration curves; weak acid-base equilibria |\n| `equilibrium` | plot | initial concentrations, K, a stress (add, remove, compress) | ICE table solved exactly; direction of shift from Q against K | yes / no | chemical equilibrium and Le Chatelier's principle, equilibrium constant and reaction quotient |\n| `crystal-lattice` | 3D | lattice type (simple cubic, body-centred, face-centred, rock salt) | atoms per cell 1, 2, 4; packing fractions pi/6, pi sqrt(3)/8, pi sqrt(2)/6; rock salt has 4 formula units per cell and 6:6 coordination | no / no | types of solids and crystal lattices, ionic bonding and lattices, metallic bonding |\n| `galvanic-cell` | board | two half-cells from a curated table; concentrations | cell potential = cathode minus anode; Nernst equation | yes / yes | galvanic and electrolytic cells; cell potential and the Nernst equation |\n| `states-of-matter` | plot with `ParticleBox` | heat added to a sample | q = m c dT on the slopes; q = m L on the plateaus | yes / yes | states of matter and phase changes |\n| `stoichiometry` | board | a balanced reaction and starting amounts | atoms balance; limiting reactant by exact rational arithmetic | no / yes | balancing chemical equations, stoichiometry and limiting reactants, the mole |\n| `stereochemistry` | 3D | a chiral molecule from a curated set beside its mirror image; swap two substituents | R or S from priorities computed by atomic number for the curated set; the mirror image and any single swap flip it | no / no | chirality and stereoisomers, optical activity and Fischer projections |\n| `conformations` | 3D | the dihedral angle of ethane or butane, seen as a Newman projection, with its energy curve | energy curve has period 120 degrees for ethane; staggered minima and eclipsed maxima; butane anti below gauche; barrier values curated and labelled approximate | no / no | conformational analysis and Newman projections |\n| `reaction-mechanism` | board with `Stepper` | a mechanism (SN2, SN1, E2, E1, electrophilic addition); step through it | atoms and total charge conserved at every step; SN2 inverts the stereocentre and SN1 gives both; the rate law's order matches the mechanism | no / no | SN1 and SN2 reactions, E1 and E2 elimination, electrophilic addition to alkenes |\n\n`ideal-gas` and `nuclear-decay` (Wave 3) list chemistry as a second subject. Organic\nchemistry's spectroscopy, nomenclature, aromaticity and carbonyl-chemistry concepts stay\nunmapped: no model here shows them faithfully.\n\n","newText":"### Wave 4: chemistry (10 models, on top of `molecule-3d` and `reaction-energy`)\n\n| Model | Stage | Student manipulates | Gate | Eq / WP | Concept families |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `atom-model` | 3D | element; isotope; ion charge | shell capacities 2 n^2; configuration by filling order with the documented exceptions held in data; average atomic mass = weighted sum | no / no | atomic structure, electron configuration, atomic orbitals, ion formation, isotopes and average atomic mass |\n| `titration` | plot | acid and base strength, concentrations, volume added | pH from the charge-balance equation, solved by bounded bisection to a stated tolerance; direction of shift from Q against K | yes / no | chemical equilibrium and Le Chatelier's principle, equilibrium constant and reaction quotient |\n| `crystal-lattice` | 3D | lattice type (simple cubic, body-centred, face-centred, rock salt) | atoms per cell 1, 2, 4; packing fractions pi/6, pi sqrt(3)/8, pi sqrt(2)/6; rock salt has 4 formula units per cell and 6:6 coordination | no / no | types of solids and crystal lattices, ionic bonding and lattices, metallic bonding |\n| `galvanic-cell` | board | two half-cells from a curated table; concentrations | cell potential = cathode minus anode; Nernst equation | yes / yes | galvanic and electrolytic cells; cell potential and the Nernst equation |\n| `states-of-matter` | plot with `ParticleBox` | heat added to a sample | q = m c dT on the slopes; q = m L on the plateaus | yes / yes | states of matter and phase changes |\n| `stoichiometry` | board | a balanced reaction and starting amounts | atoms balance; limiting reactant by exact rational arithmetic | no / yes | balancing chemical equations, stoichiometry and limiting reactants, the mole |\n| `stereochemistry` | 3D | a chiral molecule from a curated set beside its mirror image; swap two substituents | R or S from priorities stored for the curated set, each checked in tests against the atomic-number rule at the first point of difference; the mirror image and any single swap flip it | no / no | chirality and stereoisomers, optical activity and Fischer projections |\n| `conformations` | 3D | the dihedral angle of ethane or butane, seen as a Newman projection, with its energy curve | energy curve has period 120 degrees for ethane; staggered minima and eclipsed maxima; butane anti below gauche; barrier values curated and labelled approximate | no / no | conformational analysis and Newman projections |\n| `reaction-mechanism` | board with `Stepper` | a mechanism (SN2, SN1, E2, E1, electrophilic addition); step through it | atoms and total charge conserved at every step; SN2 inverts the stereocentre and SN1 gives both; the rate law's order matches the mechanism | no / no | SN1 and SN2 reactions, E1 and E2 elimination, electrophilic addition to alkenes |\n\n`ideal-gas` and `nuclear-decay` (Wave 3) list chemistry as a second subject. Organic\nchemistry's spectroscopy, nomenclature, aromaticity and carbonyl-chemistry concepts stay\nunmapped: no model here shows them faithfully.\n\n"},{"oldText":"### Stage details\n\nColour. Before Wave 1 the palette gains what the new subjects need, in one place\n(`materials.ts` and the `.viz` variables): a six-step series ramp for plots, a\npositive/negative pair, a reactant/product pair, and element colours. Where a field has a\nconvention (red oxygen, red for positive charge) the hue follows the convention and the\nlightness and saturation follow the house palette. Nothing is told apart by colour alone:\nevery coloured thing also has a label, a sign or a shape.\n\n3D models, their one glass object, and what the 2D fallback shows:\n\n| Model | Glass object | 2D fallback |\n|---|---|---|\n| `surface-3d` | the surface | contour map with the probe point and gradient arrow |\n| `molecule-3d` | the central atom | wedge-and-dash structure with bond angles labelled |\n| `solid-of-revolution` | the solid | the region and one slice in cross-section |\n| `solid-geometry` | the solid | outline drawing with dimensions labelled |\n| `conic-sections` | the cutting plane | the cut curve in its own plane with its foci |\n| `vectors-3d` | the plane the vectors span | component table beside an x-y projection |\n| `collisions` | the first cart | side view |\n| `energy-conservation` | the ball | side view with the energy bars |\n| `torque-balance` | the beam | side view |\n| `rolling-motion` | the rolling body | side view |\n| `angular-momentum` | the platform | top view |\n| `buoyancy` | the fluid | side cross-section |\n| `fluid-flow` | the pipe | side cross-section |\n| `magnetic-force` | the charge | view along the field, showing the circular path |\n| `magnetic-field-wire` | the wire | cross-section with the field circles |\n| `ideal-gas` | the box | flat box of particles |\n| `atom-model` | the nucleus | shell diagram |\n| `crystal-lattice` | the unit cell | plan view, layer by layer |\n| `stereochemistry` | the stereocentre | wedge-and-dash drawings of both mirror images |\n| `conformations` | the front carbon | Newman projection |\n| `cell-explorer` | the selected organelle | labelled cross-section |\n| `dna-structure` | one base pair | untwisted ladder |\n\nSecond stages. These models declare an `aux` stage:\n\n| Model | Main stage | Aux stage |\n|---|---|---|\n| `unit-circle` | the circle | the sine, cosine and tangent traces (plot) |\n| `kinematics-1d` | the three linked graphs | the cart on its track (board) |\n| `energy-conservation` | the track | the energy bars (board) |\n| `ideal-gas` | the box of particles | the PV diagram (plot) |\n| `states-of-matter` | the heating curve | the particles (board) |\n| `conformations` | the molecule | the energy curve (plot) |\n\nGestures. Where the stage itself is the control: `linear-transform` (drag the tips of the\ntwo basis vectors), `regression` (drag data points), `electric-field` (drag charges),\n`unit-circle` (drag the point round the circle), `triangle-solver` (drag vertices),\n`transformations-2d` (drag the figure), `conic-sections` (drag the plane's tilt). Each\nhas arrow-key nudging and mirrored number fields. Vectors in `vectors-3d` are edited by\ntheir component fields, not dragged.\n\nBoundaries that are the lesson, drawn and labelled rather than refused:\n\n| Model | Boundary | What is drawn |\n|---|---|---|\n| `refraction` | past the critical angle | the reflected ray, labelled \"total internal reflection\" |\n| `triangle-solver` | SSA with no solution, or two | no triangle with the gap shown, or both triangles |\n| `doppler` | source at or above wave speed | the wavefronts piling into a cone; frequency shown as an en dash |\n| `photoelectric-effect` | below the threshold frequency | no electrons at any intensity, labelled |\n| `linear-transform` | determinant zero | the grid collapsed onto a line, labelled \"no inverse\" |\n| `galvanic-cell` | negative cell potential | the cell labelled \"not spontaneous as drawn\" |\n| `free-body-diagram` | static friction holding | the block at rest with friction equal to the pull |\n| `ray-diagram` | object at the focal point | parallel rays, \"image at infinity\" (already built) |\n| `orbit-sim` | at or above escape speed | the open path, labelled \"unbound\" (already built) |\n\nPermanent exclusion: the \"Computer programming\" course (JavaScript drawing, HTML/CSS and\nSQL). Its concepts are language syntax and tooling; no model here shows them faithfully.\nEvery other course gets at least one mapped concept by the wave named in its pending entry.\n\n","newText":"### Stage details\n\nColour. Before Wave 1 the palette gains what the new subjects need, in one place\n(`materials.ts` and the `.viz` variables): a six-step series ramp for plots, a\npositive/negative pair, a reactant/product pair, and element colours. Where a field has a\nconvention (red oxygen, red for positive charge) the hue follows the convention and the\nlightness and saturation follow the house palette. Nothing is told apart by colour alone:\nevery coloured thing also has a label, a sign or a shape.\n\n3D models, their one glass object, and what the 2D fallback shows:\n\n| Model | Glass object | 2D fallback |\n|---|---|---|\n| `surface-3d` | the surface | contour map with the probe point and gradient arrow |\n| `molecule-3d` | the central atom | wedge-and-dash structure with bond angles labelled |\n| `solid-of-revolution` | the solid | the region and one slice in cross-section |\n| `solid-geometry` | the solid | outline drawing with dimensions labelled |\n| `conic-sections` | the cutting plane | the cut curve in its own plane with its foci |\n| `vectors-3d` | the plane the vectors span | component table beside an x-y projection |\n| `collisions` | the first cart | side view |\n| `energy-conservation` | the ball | side view with the energy bars |\n| `torque-balance` | the beam | side view |\n| `rolling-motion` | the rolling body | side view |\n| `angular-momentum` | the platform | top view |\n| `buoyancy` | the fluid | side cross-section |\n| `fluid-flow` | the pipe | side cross-section |\n| `magnetic-force` | the charge | view along the field, showing the circular path |\n| `magnetic-field-wire` | the wire | cross-section with the field circles |\n| `ideal-gas` | the box | flat box of particles |\n| `atom-model` | the nucleus | shell diagram |\n| `crystal-lattice` | the unit cell | plan view, layer by layer |\n| `stereochemistry` | the stereocentre | wedge-and-dash drawings of both mirror images |\n| `conformations` | the front carbon | Newman projection |\n| `cell-explorer` | the selected organelle | labelled cross-section |\n| `dna-structure` | one base pair | untwisted ladder |\n\nSecond stages. These models declare an `aux` stage:\n\n| Model | Main stage | Aux stage |\n|---|---|---|\n| `unit-circle` | the circle | the sine, cosine and tangent traces (plot) |\n| `kinematics-1d` | the three linked graphs | the cart on its track (board) |\n| `energy-conservation` | the track | the energy bars (board) |\n| `ideal-gas` | the box of particles | the PV diagram (plot) |\n| `states-of-matter` | the heating curve | the particles (board) |\n| `conformations` | the molecule | the energy curve (plot) |\n\nGestures. Where the stage itself is the control: `linear-transform` (drag the tips of the\ntwo basis vectors), `regression` (drag data points), `electric-field` (drag charges),\n`unit-circle` (drag the point round the circle), `triangle-solver` (drag vertices),\n`transformations-2d` (drag the figure). Each uses `DragHandle`, so each has arrow-key\nnudging and a mirrored number field. The tilt of the plane in `conic-sections` is a\nslider, because a drag there would fight with orbiting and with scrolling. Vectors in `vectors-3d` are edited by\ntheir component fields, not dragged.\n\nBoundaries that are the lesson, drawn and labelled rather than refused:\n\n| Model | Boundary | What is drawn |\n|---|---|---|\n| `refraction` | past the critical angle | the reflected ray, labelled \"total internal reflection\" |\n| `triangle-solver` | SSA with no solution, or two | no triangle with the gap shown, or both triangles |\n| `doppler` | source at or above wave speed | the wavefronts piling into a cone; frequency shown as an en dash |\n| `photoelectric-effect` | below the threshold frequency | no electrons at any intensity, labelled |\n| `linear-transform` | determinant zero | the grid collapsed onto a line, labelled \"no inverse\" |\n| `galvanic-cell` | negative cell potential | the cell labelled \"not spontaneous as drawn\" |\n| `free-body-diagram` | static friction holding | the block at rest with friction equal to the pull |\n| `ray-diagram` | object at the focal point | parallel rays, \"image at infinity\" (already built) |\n| `orbit-sim` | at or above escape speed | the open path, labelled \"unbound\" (already built) |\n\nEdges that range sampling will reach, and what each model does there:\n\n| Model | Edge | What happens |\n|---|---|---|\n| `unit-circle` | tangent at 90 degrees | the tangent line runs off the stage; the value is an en dash |\n| `linear-transform` | a matrix with no real eigenvectors (any rotation) | labelled \"no real eigenvectors\"; none drawn |\n| `regression` | every point at the same x | no line; the slope is an en dash, labelled \"vertical\" |\n| `triangle-solver` | three points in a line | the flat triangle, labelled \"no triangle\" |\n| `electric-field` | the test charge on top of a source charge | the field there is an en dash |\n| `conic-sections` | the plane through the tip of the cone | the point, line or pair of lines, labelled |\n| `taylor-series` | a centre outside the function's domain | the centre's range stops at the domain's edge |\n| `distribution`, `titration`, `galvanic-cell`, `equilibrium` | a zero spread or a zero concentration | the control's range starts above zero |\n| `distribution` | a binomial with a large count | probabilities computed in logarithms, so they stay finite |\n\nPermanent exclusion: the \"Computer programming\" course (JavaScript drawing, HTML/CSS and\nSQL). Its concepts are language syntax and tooling; no model here shows them faithfully.\nEvery other course gets at least one mapped concept by the wave named in its pending entry.\n\n"},{"oldText":"## Order and delivery\n\n- Wave 0 ships first, alone, as its own PR and version (0.3.4.0). Nothing else starts\n  until it is merged, because every later model depends on the folder shape, the generator\n  and the catalog.\n- No PR carries more than seven new models, so one person can review it. Wave 1 is one\n  PR; Wave 2 is two (6 and 5); Wave 3 is three (7, 7, 6); Wave 4 is two (5 and 5); Wave 5\n  is two (6 and 5); Wave 6 is one. Each PR is one version bump (0.3.5.0 onward), through\n  `/review` then `/ship`.\n- After Wave 1 the per-model and per-wave estimates are replaced with what Wave 1\n  actually took, before Wave 2 starts.\n- When the plan is approved, a dated note is added to `docs/DESIGN.md` recording the\n  founder's decision to build the prebuilt model library past the original ~12 types, so\n  the approved design and this plan do not contradict each other.\n- Before a wave's fan-out the integrator builds any kit the wave first needs, adds the\n  unit families it needs, creates each new model's branch with its folder scaffolded\n  (`bun run new:model <type> --concepts ...`), and writes its brief from\n  `docs/MODEL-BRIEF.md`.\n- Inside a wave the new models are built in parallel (one agent per model, each in its own\n  git worktree). A new model's branch touches only its own folder and runs\n  `bun run test <type>`, `registry.test.ts`, build, lint and `bun run sweep <type>`. Two things are not part\n  of the fan-out and are done by one agent in sequence: kits (`src/engine/kits/`) and\n  extensions to the existing 8 models, whose code lives in shared files.\n- The integrator merges the folders, answers every kit request, deletes the exclusion\n  entries the wave satisfied, then runs `bun run test`, `bun run test:catalog`, build,\n  lint and the full `bun run sweep` on the combined tree.\n- Waves run in the order above. The work can stop cleanly at any wave boundary with\n  everything shipped up to that point. They are replaced after 0b and again\nafter Wave 1.\n\n"},{"oldText":"## Risks\n\n1. **Factual content outside physics.** Bond angles, standard potentials, the codon table,\n   torsional barriers, mechanism steps and pathway steps come from reference knowledge, not\n   from a derivation. Each such table gets cross-checks in tests where one exists (64\n   codons, angle sums, charge balance, net yields), is checked against its cited source by\n   a second agent, and is listed in the PR for a founder spot-check.\n2. **Depth against breadth.** 65 models at the physics-model bar is a lot of surface to\n   keep correct. The registry tests, the per-model closed-form tests and the sweep are what\n   keep quality from decaying as the count grows.\n3. **Simulated models** (`ParticleBox`, `natural-selection`, `sampling`) can show a picture\n   that disagrees with the number beside it. Rule: every readout is closed-form; the\n   animation is seeded and only illustrative; tests pin the seed.\n4. **Bundle size.** The Try page loads the small meta index eagerly and one model's maths,\n   presets and widget on demand; the topics page loads one subject's catalog. Checked in\n   the build output each wave.\n5. **The DESIGN.md target was ~12 spec types and \"validate before building\".** This plan\n   goes to 73. That is the founder's call to make; it is flagged, not assumed.\n6. **Usage logging needs one founder action.** The logging code ships in Wave 0, but it\n   records nothing until the founder runs `supabase/model_events.sql`. Until then the\n   breadth is unmeasured.\n7. **Search engines will not index concept pages.** The app uses hash routes, which are\n   not indexed as separate pages. See \"Not in scope\".\n\n","newText":"## Risks\n\n1. **Factual content outside physics.** Bond angles, standard potentials, the codon table,\n   torsional barriers, mechanism steps and pathway steps come from reference knowledge, not\n   from a derivation. The registry tests, the per-model closed-form tests and the sweep are what\n   keep quality from decaying as the count grows.\n3. **Simulated models** (`ParticleBox`, `natural-selection`, `sampling`) can show a picture\n   that disagrees with the number beside it. Rule: every readout is closed-form; the\n   animation is seeded and only illustrative; tests pin the seed.\n4. **Bundle size.** The Try page loads the small meta index eagerly and one model's maths,\n   presets and widget on demand; the topics page loads the catalog. `check:bundle` holds\n   the line on every PR.\n5. **The DESIGN.md target was ~12 spec types and \"validate before building\".** This plan\n   goes to 73. That is the founder's call to make; it is flagged, not assumed.\n6. **Usage logging needs one founder action.** The logging code ships in Wave 0, but it\n   records nothing until the founder runs `supabase/model_events.sql`. Until then the\n   breadth is unmeasured.\n7. **The frame's contract is new.** Every new model depends on how a widget and\n   `ModelFrame` share the spec. It is proven on `projectile` and `function-grapher` in\n   Wave 0, before any new model is built; a change after fan-out touches every model.\n8. **The kits may not fit their consumers.** `PlotStage` starts as 126 lines and must\n   serve about 25 models. Each kit is built against its hardest consumers first, and a\n   kit request is the route for the rest.\n9. **Search engines will not index concept pages.** The app uses hash routes, which are\n   not indexed as separate pages. Deeper per\n  concept, far narrower. Target: this plan (`docs/plans/model-buildout.md`), the same file that holds the report.
Review of a plan: every "will" below is a proposal, and every claim about today's code
was read in the repo at 58fc6ad.

#### Step 0: Scope Challenge

What already exists, and what the plan does with it:

| Sub-problem | Existing code | Plan |
|---|---|---|
| One layout for every model | Each widget draws its own whole page: word-problem bar, stage, readouts, transport and `EquationPanel` (`src/engine/widgets/ProjectileSim.tsx:300-400`), in shared `.pmodel` classes (`src/engine/visualize.css`) | extracted into `ModelFrame` and slots |
| Solve-for-any-variable | `useEquationPanel(set, base, setBase, onInteract)` (`src/engine/useEquationPanel.ts`) already takes the base values and a setter from outside | reused unchanged; the frame supplies both |
| 3D scaffold | `Scene3D.tsx`, `primitives.tsx`, `materials.ts`, `hasWebGL.ts` | reused; the probe is memoized |
| Plot | `widgets/FunctionGrapher.tsx` (126 lines) | becomes `PlotStage` |
| Curriculum gate | `src/data/curriculum/build.ts` validates and exits non-zero | grows the mapping gate and the catalog |
| Tests | 3 standalone scripts, 153 checks, covering the circuit solver, orbit, lens and the topics column list | runner, `testkit.ts`, and tests for the 5 models that have none |
| Routing | `src/Root.tsx` switches on the hash; `src/main.tsx:13` reloads the page on every hash change | in-place navigation with an error boundary |
| Word problems | 7 hand-written regex parsers (`wordProblems.ts`, `projectileWordProblem.ts`) | a shared quantity reader; per-model role assignment |

Complexity check: Wave 0 alone proposes roughly 60 new files and a dozen new modules (the
frame, seven kits, the generator, the plugin, the scaffold, the sweep, usage logging, the
catalog build). That is far past the 8-file, 2-module line. Under /autoplan the scope is
not reduced. The structure question is answered with a smaller arrangement of the same
scope: four stacked PRs instead of one, one loader per model instead of three, one
catalog file instead of five.

Search check: no new infrastructure beyond a Vite plugin (its `buildStart` and watcher
hooks are built in), Playwright, and one Postgres table behind PostgREST. Web search was
not run in this phase; three facts the design leans on come from general knowledge and
are checked by the plan's own tests rather than assumed: Postgres applies a row-security
check before a CHECK constraint (the smoke script proves the probe's code), Bun is
available in the Vercel build (0b's preview build proves it), and a browser's limit on
live 3D contexts (the sweep loads one model per page either way).

TODOS cross-reference: the repo has no `TODOS.md`. This phase creates it.

Distribution check: nothing new is published. The deploy is the existing Vercel build,
which must now run the generator.

Scope Challenge result: scope accepted as-is (MODE: FULL_REVIEW).

#### Dual voices

CODEX SAYS (eng, architecture challenge): unavailable. The `codex` CLI is not installed.

CLAUDE SUBAGENT (eng, independent review), input `eng 830556a2…cf54`, read in full.
Verdict: "unusually thorough on process, but not ready for the fan-out." 36 findings: 1
critical, 9 high, 26 medium or low. It confirmed the plan's own counts (65 models, 23
courses, 22 3D models, the `specType` drift). Each finding was checked against the code
before a decision.

| # | Sev. | Finding | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | critical | The widget-to-frame contract is undefined: one `{ spec }` component cannot be placed into the frame's regions, take a parser's patch, or show a refusal beside a control | accepted: the frame owns the live spec; `set`; slots; frame-rendered equations and word problems; the six legacy widgets slot-wrapped. Confirmed in `ProjectileSim.tsx` |
| A2 | high | Wave 0 is one PR that moves and reformats untested code while adding its first tests | accepted: four stacked PRs; tests of today's behaviour land first |
| A3 | high | "Only its own folder" and "core is pure" are conventions | accepted: ESLint import and global rules, a branch-scope check, colour check on TSX |
| A4 | medium | Gate and renderer sharing one core cannot be seen in the types | accepted: `derive` |
| A5 | high | Generator: no atomic writes, concurrent runs, bypassed by plain `vite`, stubs can reach production | accepted: write-on-change and rename, plugin `buildStart`, check mode on a production deploy, stubs left out of production |
| A6 | medium | Per-subject catalogs contradict tab counts and cross-subject search | accepted: one catalog file |
| A7 | low | Three lazy chunks per model buy nothing | accepted: one loader per model |
| A8 | medium | Nothing tests what a model's chunk drags in | accepted: no kits barrel, split `ParticleBox`, `check:bundle`. Confirmed: `validate.ts:11` imports mathjs |
| A9 | medium | Two expression checks would coexist | accepted: `function-grapher` rebuilt on `safeExpression` |
| A10 | medium | Superseded text stays in the accepted records | accepted in part: the records cannot be edited; a closing section says the plan's sections win; `subjects` made plural in `planned.ts` |
| A11 | medium | Scripts, the curriculum build and `api/` are not typechecked | accepted: `tsconfig.scripts.json`; the generator in TypeScript. Confirmed in `tsconfig.node.json` and `tsconfig.app.json:25` |
| E1 | high | "Controls clamp" is never verified; ten named divide-by-zero cases | accepted: `ranges` as data, range sampling in the registry test, an edge table |
| E2 | medium | `surface-3d` would refuse natural inputs | accepted: holes, not refusal |
| E3 | medium | WebGL loss underspecified; the probe leaks a context per call | accepted. Confirmed in `hasWebGL.ts` |
| E4 | medium | Removing the reload changes every route's lifecycle; no error boundary anywhere | accepted: route error boundary, guarded decode, skeleton for `c` without a type, focus on navigation. Confirmed in `main.tsx:13` and `Root.tsx:25` |
| E5 | medium | The pinned stage breaks in landscape and with the keyboard; 2D drags fight scrolling | accepted: small-viewport height, unpin under 480px, `DragHandle`, conic tilt as a slider |
| E6 | medium | A model with every concept declined is unreachable and passes; course order unchecked | accepted |
| E7 | medium | Parallel worktrees can pick the same port; the sweep can attach to another server | accepted: port from the path, identity check |
| E8 | low | `fmt` flips between "3" and "3.00"; live regions flood; guarded storage read; locale sort; static below kinetic | accepted, all five |
| T1 | high | The sweep can pass without 3D ever drawing | accepted: one model per page, which renderer drew, real WebGL-off run, production build in CI, bad-text scan |
| T2 | high | No tests across control ranges | accepted with E1 |
| T3 | medium | No automated UI tests | accepted: a Playwright spec suite with a fixture model and an accessibility scan (taste: a second new dev dependency) |
| T4 | high | The table's rules are the only new write boundary and have no test | accepted: smoke script |
| T5 | medium | A test file with zero checks passes; no timeout | accepted |

| S2 | high | `model_events`: a signed-in student's rows fail or carry identity; rules cannot evolve; the probe's code; forgery; `engaged` semantics | accepted: plain `fetch` with the public key, drop-and-recreate rules, a named rule for the probe, tight checks, a delta `engaged` |

#### Section 1: Architecture

```
                         model folders  src/engine/models/<type>/
        meta.ts   core.ts   presets.ts   concepts.ts   equations.ts  parse.ts  Widget.tsx
           │         │          │             │             │           │          │
           └────┬────┴──────────┴─────────────┘             └─────┬─────┴──────────┘
                │   scripts/gen.ts  (Vite plugin at start; hooks for test, lint, typecheck)
     ┌──────────┼───────────────────────┬─────────────────────────┐
     ▼          ▼                       ▼                         ▼
 meta.gen.ts  pure.gen.ts ──► curriculum build.ts ──► catalog.gen.json     load.gen.ts
 (eager)      (build, tests)   validateMappings        (topics, Try header)  (one loader
     │              │          gate: normal / check          │                per model)
     │              ▼                                         │                    │
     │        registry.test.ts                                │                    │
     │        (presets, ranges, Eq/WP)                        │                    │
     ▼                                                        ▼                    ▼
 specTypes.ts ◄── planned.ts              Root (hash router, error boundary)
 coverage page                              │            │              │
                                            ▼            ▼              ▼
                                       TopicsPage   VisualizePanel   landing, sign-in
                                       (catalog)        │
                                                        ▼
                                        resolver (path, ?c=) ──► ModelFrame
                                                                   │  owns the live spec
                                    ┌──────────────┬───────────────┼────────────────┐
                                    ▼              ▼               ▼                ▼
                              core.validate   equation panel   word-problem     Widget (spec, set)
                              core.derive     (useEquationPanel)  bar (parse)   Frame.Stage / Aux /
                                    │                                           Controls / Readouts
                                    ▼                                                │
                              controls kit ◄─────────── kits: PlotStage, BoardStage, Scene3D,
                              (ranges, field errors)    Transport, Stepper, ParticleBox,
                                                        DragHandle, ExpressionInput
                                    │
                                    ▼
                              usage.ts ── plain fetch, public key ──► model_events (insert only)
```

Coupling: models depend on the contract and the kits and on nothing else, and lint
enforces it. The frame depends on the registry, never on a model by name. The only
shared mutable thing during a wave is the kits, which only the integrator edits.
Single point of failure: the frame's contract (risk 7). Security boundary: one write
path to the outside (`model_events`), and one server endpoint, now off.

One realistic production failure per new path, and whether the plan handles it:

| Path | Failure | Handled by |
|---|---|---|
| lazy model loader | a deploy replaces chunk files while a page is open | "This model didn't load." with a reload; browser spec |
| catalog load | the request fails | header falls back to the model label; topics shows a retry |
| address resolver | a malformed `%` in the address | treated as an unknown type; resolver test row |
| any route | a throw in a page | route error boundary; browser spec |
| WebGL | context lost on a phone returning from the background | one restore, then the 2D view |
| usage insert | the table is missing or its rules refuse the row | dropped; five-state status; smoke script |
| generator | two runs at once; a half-written file | rename into place; write only on change |
| production deploy | a stub or an unanswered kit request reaches main | check mode on a production deploy |

Three callers of identical behaviour. Decision: deleted in favour of the shared `fmt` (in the plan, 0c). About 15 lines removed, none added.
- [P2] (confidence: 9/10) `src/engine/wordProblems.ts:32,82,116,170,204,263` and `src/engine/projectileWordProblem.ts:30`: seven `export function parse…Problem` with their own number-and-unit matching; about 40 more are planned. Decision: a shared quantity reader in the kits; each `parse.ts` assigns roles (T6). The seven existing parsers are not rewritten, except `projectile`'s, which moves.
- [P3] (confidence: 9/10) `src/engine/physicsModels.test.ts:19-24` (`const approx`, `const check`) is repeated in `circuitNetwork.test.ts`. Decision: `testkit.ts`; the two legacy files keep their own until touched.

Suppressed (confidence under 5, appendix only): the plugin's watcher may fire twice per

Regression rule: Wave 0 moves and reformats four models that have no tests. The
regression contract is the 0a PR: closed-form assertions and the exact readout text of
each default preset, written against today's code and required to pass unchanged
through 0b. The one intended difference, the `fmt` change in 0c, updates those strings
in the same PR that changes the format, so the diff shows every readout that moved.
Flagged CRITICAL and carried into the plan.

Tests made obsolete: `src/topics/concepts.test.ts`'s live-table column check (the query
it guards is removed in 0c). Its `tryHref` cases move to the catalog's link tests.

Test plan artifact: `~/.gstack/projects/patelmemail0-sys-Saras/madhavpatel-feat-model-buildout-eng-review-test-plan-20261001-162852.md`.

#### Section 4: Performance

- [P2] (confidence: 7/10) Frame-owned spec means a playing model calls `set` about 60 times a second, and each change runs `validate` and `derive`. Today's widgets already set state per frame (`ProjectileSim.tsx`, `setT`), so the cost is the same order; `derive` must stay closed-form and cheap, and the live region is silent while playing. In the plan.
- [P2] (confidence: 8/10) `mathjs` is a large dependency and `validate.ts:11` imports it, so all six adapter chunks would carry it. Moving the function-grapher check out, and `check:bundle`, keep it to expression models. In the plan.
- [P2] (confidence: 7/10) 400 particles as React-rendered SVG at 60 frames a second would stutter on phones. The flat renderer draws to a canvas. In the plan.
- [P3] (confidence: 7/10) One catalog of 734 rows is about 65KB before compression, loaded once and lazily. Search is a substring scan of 734 titles per keystroke: no index needed.
- [P3] (confidence: 6/10) Medium confidence, verify: range sampling across 73 models, their presets and controls could take seconds. Cap the seeded points per control so the registry test stays under about five seconds.
- [P3] (confidence: 7/10) The CI sweep is 73 models at two widths, one page load each: a few minutes. Running only changed models is deferred.

#### Failure modes

| Path | Realistic failure | Test | Handling | Seen by the user as |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| moved maths | a reformat or move changes a number | 0a pinned tests | n/a | n/a (caught before merge) |
| range corner | a control at its end gives a non-finite readout | range sampling | `derive` returns "undefined here" | an en dash, labelled |
| lazy chunk | chunk missing after a deploy | browser spec | notice and reload | "This model didn't load." |
| widget throw | a model crashes | browser spec (fixture) | stage error boundary | "This model hit an error." |
| route throw | resolver or header crashes | browser spec | route error boundary | a one-line notice |
| WebGL loss | context lost | sweep (real WebGL off) | restore once, then 2D | "Showing the 2D view" |
| usage insert | table missing, rule refusal, offline | unit + smoke script | dropped | nothing (by design); founder sees the status |
| stale exclusion, stub, kit request | reaches main | check mode | production deploy fails | nothing ships |
| wrong server | sweep attaches to another worktree | identity check | sweep fails | n/a |
| typed expression | hostile or odd input | `safeExpression` tests | refused with the token named | a message under the field |

Critical gaps (no test, no handling, silent): 0.

#### NOT in scope (eng)

- A rate limit on `api/spec.ts`: the endpoint is off; a limit is a condition of enabling it.
- Converting the six legacy widgets to frame-owned state: they are slot-wrapped.
- Running only changed models' sweep in CI: fast enough at 73 models.
- A scheduled clean-up of `model_events`: the statement is in the report file.
- Error reporting from students' browsers: already deferred by the CEO phase.
- Rewriting the six existing word-problem parsers onto the quantity reader: untested gain.

#### Worktree parallelization strategy

| Step | Modules touched | Depends on |
|---|---|---|
| 0a tests, CI, endpoint off | `scripts/`, `src/engine/` tests, `api/`, `.github/` | none |
| 0b folders, generator, catalog | `src/engine/models/`, `scripts/`, `src/data/curriculum/` | 0a |
| 0c frame, kits, routing, topics | `src/engine/kits/`, `src/engine/`, `src/topics/`, `src/` root | 0b |
| 0d usage, sweep, scaffold, docs | `src/lib/`, `supabase/`, `scripts/`, `docs/`, `e2e/` | 0c |
| kits for a wave | `src/engine/kits/` | Wave 0 |
| five legacy extensions (Wave 1) | `src/engine/` shared files | Wave 0 |
| each new model | `src/engine/models/<type>/` | its wave's kits |

Lanes: Wave 0 is one lane, in sequence (each PR builds on the last). Inside 0d, three
workstreams touch different modules and can run in parallel worktrees: usage logging
(`src/lib/`, `supabase/`), the sweep and browser specs (`scripts/`, `e2e/`), and the docs
and scaffold (`docs/`, `scripts/new-model.ts`); they share `package.json`, so one agent
merges that file. From Wave 1 on: Lane A is the kits and Lane B the legacy extensions,
each in sequence and independent of each other; then one lane per model, four to six at
once. Conflict flags: `package.json` and `bun.lock` (integrator only; `check:branch`
fails a model branch that touches them), and `src/engine/kits/` (integrator only).
